{"parentUuid":"8d86bb79-d390-4411-b8ce-5aa1b415a52f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d7b89794-41f0-46fe-9a20-6d198f8f624f","agentId":"a5ea667e8aa34be02","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PLtFNMLrdsNrFKMpZzhH5e","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/-\n2\tPhase 5q.B: assembling `8 ∣ σ` from the classification ([E3] in `Phase5qB_LabNotebook.md`).\n3\t\n4\tThe classification of even unimodular lattices says every such form is congruent to a block-diagonal sum\n5\t`E₈^a ⊕ (−E₈)^b ⊕ H^c`. Granting that normal form, `8 ∣ σ` is now PURELY the signature calculus built in\n6\t`E8Signature` / `LatticeSignatureCongr` / `BlockSignature` / `LatticeSigBlock` / `GeneratorNondeg`: each\n7\tgenerator has `8 ∣ σ`, block sums preserve it, and congruence/reindexing preserve `latticeSig`. This module\n8\trecords exactly those closure facts — the inductive content of the assembly. What remains to make `8 ∣ σ`\n9\t*unconditional* is solely the classification's EXISTENCE statement (every even unimodular form is congruent\n10\tto such a normal form), whose two irreducible inputs — Hasse–Minkowski (indefinite represents 0) and\n11\ttheta-modularity (definite `8 ∣ rank`) — have no Mathlib substrate and are tracked as the remaining gap.\n12\t\n13\t* `eight_dvd_latticeSig_e8lit` / `…_neg_e8lit` / `…_hyp` — the three generators each satisfy `8 ∣ σ`\n14\t  (`σ = 8, −8, 0`).\n15\t* `eight_dvd_latticeSigOf_fromBlocks` — block sums of nondegenerate `8 ∣ σ` blocks satisfy `8 ∣ σ` (the\n16\t  inductive step).\n17\t* `eight_dvd_latticeSig_congr` / `eight_dvd_latticeSigOf_reindex` — `8 ∣ σ` is invariant under integer\n18\t  congruence and reindexing (so it transfers across the classification isomorphism).\n19\t\n20\tAll proofs are kernel-pure (`propext`/`Classical.choice`/`Quot.sound` only); no `native_decide`, no\n21\t`maxHeartbeats`, no axiom.\n22\t-/\n23\t\n24\timport Mathlib\n25\timport SKEFTHawking.AlgebraicRokhlin\n26\timport SKEFTHawking.E8Signature\n27\timport SKEFTHawking.LatticeSignatureCongr\n28\timport SKEFTHawking.BlockSignature\n29\timport SKEFTHawking.GeneratorNondeg\n30\timport SKEFTHawking.LatticeSigBlock\n31\t\n32\tnamespace SKEFTHawking\n33\t\n34\topen Matrix\n35\t\n36\t/-! ## Generators: each has `8 ∣ σ` -/\n37\t\n38\t/-- `8 ∣ σ(E₈)` (`σ = 8`). -/\n39\ttheorem eight_dvd_latticeSig_e8lit : (8 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSig E8lit := by rw [e8lit_latticeSig]\n40\t\n41\t/-- `8 ∣ σ(−E₈)` (`σ = −8`). -/\n42\ttheorem eight_dvd_latticeSig_neg_e8lit : (8 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSig (-E8lit) := by\n43\t  rw [neg_e8lit_latticeSig]; norm_num\n44\t\n45\t/-- `8 ∣ σ(H)` (`σ = 0`). -/\n46\ttheorem eight_dvd_latticeSig_hyp : (8 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSig Hyp := by rw [hyp_latticeSig]; norm_num\n47\t\n48\t/-! ## Closure under block sums, congruence, and reindexing -/\n49\t\n50\t/-- **Inductive step:** a block-diagonal sum of two nondegenerate blocks, each with `8 ∣ σ`, has `8 ∣ σ`. -/\n51\ttheorem eight_dvd_latticeSigOf_fromBlocks {na nb : ℕ} (A : Matrix (Fin na) (Fin na) ℤ)\n52\t    (B : Matrix (Fin nb) (Fin nb) ℤ)\n53\t    (hA : (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap'.radical = ⊥)\n54\t    (hB : (B.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap'.radical = ⊥)\n55\t    (hdA : (8 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSigOf A) (hdB : (8 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSigOf B) :\n56\t    (8 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSigOf (Matrix.fromBlocks A 0 0 B) := by\n57\t  rw [latticeSigOf_fromBlocks A B hA hB]\n58\t  exact Dvd.dvd.add hdA hdB\n59\t\n60\t/-- `8 ∣ σ` is invariant under integer congruence `M ↦ Pᵀ M P` (`P ∈ GL(ℤ)`) — so it transfers from the\n61\tclassification normal form back to `M`. -/\n62\ttheorem eight_dvd_latticeSig_congr {n : ℕ} (M P : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ) (hP : IsUnit P.det) :\n63\t    (8 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSig (Pᵀ * M * P) ↔ (8 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSig M := by\n64\t  rw [latticeSig_congr M P hP]\n65\t\n66\t/-- `8 ∣ σ` is invariant under reindexing the lattice basis. -/\n67\ttheorem eight_dvd_latticeSigOf_reindex {ι ι' : Type*} [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] [Fintype ι']\n68\t    [DecidableEq ι'] (e : ι ≃ ι') (M : Matrix ι ι ℤ) :\n69\t    (8 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSigOf (Matrix.reindex e e M) ↔ (8 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSigOf M := by\n70\t  rw [latticeSigOf_reindex]\n71\t\n72\t/-! ## Bridge to the Rokhlin conclusion -/\n73\t\n74\t/-- **The Rokhlin composition in `latticeSig` terms.** For an even unimodular form, the algebraic bound\n75\t`8 ∣ latticeSig M` (van der Blij — the classification target) together with the topological factor\n76\t`2 ∣ latticeSig M / 8` (Â-genus even / `Arf(q̄)=0`) gives `16 ∣ latticeSig M`, kernel-pure. This bridges the\n77\tsignature machinery of this Phase to the wired Rokhlin conclusion: the only non-geometric input is the\n78\tsingle, precisely-isolated `8 ∣ latticeSig M`. -/\n79\ttheorem sixteen_dvd_latticeSig_of_eight_dvd_of_topo {n : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ)\n80\t    (h8 : (8 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSig M) (htopo : (2 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSig M / 8) :\n81\t    (16 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSig M :=\n82\t  rokhlin_from_serre_plus_topology (latticeSig M) h8 htopo\n83\t\n84\tend SKEFTHawking\n85\t"}]},"uuid":"97f02f1c-29e3-4578-be5a-3fbc1f3043b1","timestamp":"2026-08-15T19:38:35.990Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8d86bb79-d390-4411-b8ce-5aa1b415a52f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/johnroehm/Programming/PythonEnvironments/Physics/Fluid-Based-Physics-Research/SK_EFT_Hawking","sessionId":"6cf37aa2-f1c7-4e33-a712-f17c2d75d99b","version":"2.1.229","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"swift-singing-llama"}
{"parentUuid":"d23a9ee9-b306-4703-8101-fe6cb60b8dae","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d7b89794-41f0-46fe-9a20-6d198f8f624f","agentId":"a5ea667e8aa34be02","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01RAknZKAiuZP1uNv6B2U7K4","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/-\n2\tPhase 5q.B Waves B3/B4: smooth-spin 4-manifold interface and the wired `16 ∣ σ`\n3\t\n4\tINTERFACE-FIRST (roadmap Wave B3). A closed smooth spin 4-manifold contributes exactly the\n5\tdata the spectra-free Rokhlin derivation needs:\n6\t  • its intersection form is **even unimodular** (Wu's formula + Poincaré duality — topological),\n7\t  • the extra factor of two `2 ∣ σ/8` (Â-genus even / vanishing of the GEOMETRIC Guillou–Marin Arf of a\n8\t    characteristic SURFACE — the irreducible topological input, Atiyah–Singer index / Freedman–Kirby; this\n9\t    is what genuinely distinguishes smooth from topological, per Freedman's E₈ manifold). NOTE (2026-06-13):\n10\t    this is NOT the lattice `Arf(redQuad)`, which is identically 0 on even unimodular forms (the `σ/8 ≡ Arf(q̄)`\n11\t    lattice bridge is FALSE — E₈ has `Arf=0` but `σ/8=1`; see `RokhlinArfNoGo.lean`).\n12\t\n13\tThe algebraic bound `8 ∣ σ` (van der Blij) is **no longer carried as interface data** — it is now a\n14\tkernel-pure THEOREM (`RokhlinHMRankFour.eight_dvd_latticeSig`), derived from even-unimodularity via the\n15\tclassification `E₈^a ⊕ (−E₈)^b ⊕ H^c` (existence DISCHARGED: Hasse–Minkowski `[HM]` via the rank-4\n16\tquaternary route + theta-modularity `[Θ]` for the definite case).\n17\t\n18\tThe signature here is the genuine `latticeSig form` (`LatticeSignature.lean`), NOT a free integer\n19\tparameter: a manifold's signature *is* the signature of its intersection form. From these data,\n20\t`16 ∣ latticeSig form` is a kernel-pure THEOREM via `RokhlinHMRankFour.sixteen_dvd_latticeSig`\n21\t(= `rokhlin_from_serre_plus_topology` on `latticeSig`, with `8 ∣ σ` now proved) — NO global Rokhlin\n22\thypothesis, NO new axiom.\n23\t\n24\tDEPENDENCY GRAPH (anti-circularity): the derivation routes\n25\t  even-unimodular (Wu) ─→ [HM]+[Θ] ─→ 8 ∣ σ (van der Blij, PROVED)  ─┐\n26\t                                                                     ├─→ 16 ∣ σ\n27\t  2 ∣ σ/8 (Â even, topological)  ─────────────────────────────────┘\n28\tIt does NOT use Anderson–Brown–Peterson or Rokhlin's theorem itself as input (Rokhlin's theorem\n29\t*is* the conclusion `16 ∣ σ`); the `2 ∣ σ/8` field is the more primitive index-theoretic fact.\n30\t\n31\tSTATUS (2026-06-08; accuracy-corrected 2026-07-20): `8 ∣ σ` is DISCHARGED and the `eight_dvd` field has\n32\tbeen DROPPED. The two irreducible INPUT pieces landed: Hasse–Minkowski\n33\t(`RokhlinHMRankFour.hasWeakIsotropicVector`, all ranks; rank-4 frontier via binary Hilbert reciprocity)\n34\tand theta-modularity (definite `8 ∣ rank`). ⚠ The full classification EXISTENCE statement (every even\n35\tunimodular form ≅ `E₈^a ⊕ (−E₈)^b ⊕ H^c`) is NOT itself in-tree: the σ = 0 case IS\n36\t(`HyperbolicNormalForm.exists_hyperbolic_congr`, pure-H blocks), but the definite branch stops at the\n37\t`8 ∣ rank` DIVISIBILITY — the E₈-identification of the definite part (equivalently the stable\n38\tclassification with mixed signature) is the open K8b interior brick (see\n39\t`docs/dev-loops/Phase5qH/KUMMER_K4K10_DESIGN.md` §K8b). The **only remaining tracked\n40\thypothesis is `topo`** (`2 ∣ σ/8`), which is irreducibly topological (not an axiom, not algebraic).\n41\t\n42\tSee docs/roadmaps/Phase5qB_SpectraFreeSpinBordism_Roadmap.md (Waves B3, B4) and\n43\tdocs/roadmaps/Phase5qB_LabNotebook.md.\n44\t-/\n45\t\n46\timport SKEFTHawking.AlgebraicRokhlin\n47\timport SKEFTHawking.LatticeSignature\n48\timport SKEFTHawking.RokhlinClassification\n49\timport SKEFTHawking.RokhlinBridge\n50\timport SKEFTHawking.RokhlinHMRankFour\n51\timport SKEFTHawking.KitaevSixteenFold\n52\t\n53\tnamespace SKEFTHawking\n54\t\n55\t/-- A closed smooth spin 4-manifold, carrying exactly the data the Rokhlin derivation consumes:\n56\t    its even-unimodular intersection form and the topological factor-of-two. Its signature is\n57\t    `latticeSig form` (see `SmoothSpinManifold4.sig`), not a free parameter.\n58\t\n59\t    The algebraic bound `8 ∣ σ` is **no longer an interface field** — it is now a kernel-pure theorem\n60\t    (`eight_dvd_latticeSig`, via the discharged Hasse–Minkowski `hasIsotropicVector` + theta-modularity),\n61\t    so a smooth spin 4-manifold is determined by its (even-unimodular) form and the genuinely topological\n62\t    factor `2 ∣ σ/8` alone. -/\n63\tstructure SmoothSpinManifold4 where\n64\t  /-- dimension of `H²(M; ℤ)` (rank of the intersection lattice). -/\n65\t  rank : ℕ\n66\t  /-- the intersection form on `H²(M; ℤ)`. -/\n67\t  form : Matrix (Fin rank) (Fin rank) ℤ\n68\t  /-- spin ⟹ the intersection form is even unimodular (Wu's formula + Poincaré duality). -/\n69\t  even_unimod : IsEvenUnimodular form\n70\t  /-- the topological factor of two: `2 ∣ σ/8` — the Â-genus-even / geometric Guillou–Marin\n71\t      characteristic-surface Arf-vanishing (Freedman–Kirby), the single irreducible topological input.\n72\t      NOT the lattice `Arf(redQuad)` (identically 0; the lattice bridge is false — `RokhlinArfNoGo.lean`). -/\n73\t  topo : (2 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSig form / 8\n74\t\n75\t/-- The signature `σ(M)` of a smooth spin 4-manifold is the genuine signature of its intersection form. -/\n76\tnoncomputable def SmoothSpinManifold4.sig (M : SmoothSpinManifold4) : ℤ := latticeSig M.form\n77\t\n78\t/-- The algebraic half: `8 ∣ σ` for a smooth spin 4-manifold — now an unconditional theorem (van der Blij,\n79\t    derived from even-unimodularity via the discharged [HM] input), not a carried hypothesis. -/\n80\ttheorem SmoothSpinManifold4.eight_dvd_sig (M : SmoothSpinManifold4) : 8 ∣ M.sig :=\n81\t  eight_dvd_latticeSig M.rank M.form M.even_unimod\n82\t\n83\t/-- **Rokhlin's theorem, wired:** `16 ∣ σ(M)` for every closed smooth spin 4-manifold — a kernel-pure\n84\t    theorem derived from the even-unimodular form and the topological factor alone, with no global Rokhlin\n85\t    hypothesis, no assumed `8 ∣ σ`, and no new axiom. -/\n86\ttheorem SmoothSpinManifold4.rokhlin (M : SmoothSpinManifold4) : 16 ∣ M.sig :=\n87\t  sixteen_dvd_latticeSig M.form M.even_unimod M.topo\n88\t\n89\t/-- **`sixteen_convergence` without the Rokhlin hypothesis.** The unconditional companion to\n90\t    `RokhlinBridge.sixteen_convergence_full`: the `16 ∣ σ` conjunct is now a *theorem*\n91\t    (`SmoothSpinManifold4.rokhlin`), not an assumed input. -/\n92\ttheorem sixteen_convergence_unconditional :\n93\t    (∑ f : SMFermion, components f) = 16 ∧\n94\t    (16 : ZMod 16) = 0 ∧\n95\t    (∀ M : SmoothSpinManifold4, (16 : ℤ) ∣ M.sig) ∧\n96\t    (∀ ν : ℤ, Kitaev16.kitaevCentralCharge (ν + 16)\n97\t        = Kitaev16.kitaevCentralCharge ν + 8) :=\n98\t  ⟨total_components_with_nu_R, by decide,\n99\t   fun M => M.rokhlin, Kitaev16.kitaevCentralCharge_period16⟩\n100\t\n101\tend SKEFTHawking\n102\t"}]},"uuid":"3ed01315-4ccf-4025-a38a-4c3f08d91e16","timestamp":"2026-08-15T19:38:35.990Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d23a9ee9-b306-4703-8101-fe6cb60b8dae","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/johnroehm/Programming/PythonEnvironments/Physics/Fluid-Based-Physics-Research/SK_EFT_Hawking","sessionId":"6cf37aa2-f1c7-4e33-a712-f17c2d75d99b","version":"2.1.229","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"swift-singing-llama"}

{"parentUuid":"dfd36d3d-2746-41f7-b340-de43a2a4a552","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d7b89794-41f0-46fe-9a20-6d198f8f624f","agentId":"a5ea667e8aa34be02","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01X1Um4GR851eKLKkZt7wo2D","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/-\n2\t# `8 ∣ σ` and `16 ∣ σ` for even unimodular forms, with [Θ] discharged\n3\t\n4\t`VanDerBlijReduction.eight_dvd_latticeSig_of_HM_of_Theta` reduces `8 ∣ latticeSig` for every even\n5\tunimodular form to two classical inputs: [HM] (indefinite ⟹ primitive isotropic vector) and [Θ]\n6\t(definite ⟹ `8 ∣ σ`). The [Θ] input is now a **theorem**\n7\t(`ThetaDefiniteDischarge.eight_dvd_latticeSig_of_definite`, via theta-modularity), so this file composes it\n8\tin: the even-unimodular `8 ∣ σ` and `16 ∣ σ` results now depend on **only [HM]** (and the topological\n9\tfactor `2 ∣ σ/8` for the `16` version).\n10\t\n11\tThis isolates the *single* remaining mathematical gap to [HM] = the Hasse–Minkowski local–global statement\n12\tfor even unimodular forms (under construction: `HilbertSymbolReal`, `PadicUnitResidue`, `HilbertSymbolPadic`).\n13\tDependency graph (anti-circularity): `eight_dvd_rank` (theta-modularity, no Rokhlin/ABP input) → [Θ]; [HM]\n14\t(Hilbert-symbol Hasse–Minkowski, no Rokhlin/ABP input) → here. Kernel-pure, no new axioms.\n15\t-/\n16\t\n17\timport Mathlib\n18\timport SKEFTHawking.VanDerBlijReduction\n19\timport SKEFTHawking.ThetaDefiniteDischarge\n20\timport SKEFTHawking.LatticeContent\n21\t\n22\tnamespace SKEFTHawking\n23\t\n24\topen Matrix QuadraticForm\n25\t\n26\t/-- Abbreviation for the [HM] hypothesis: every *indefinite* even unimodular form has a primitive\n27\tisotropic vector. -/\n28\tabbrev HasIsotropicVectorHyp : Prop :=\n29\t  ∀ {m : ℕ} (A : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) ℤ), IsEvenUnimodular A →\n30\t    0 < sigPos (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap' →\n31\t    0 < sigNeg (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap' →\n32\t    ∃ v : Fin m → ℤ, IsPrimitiveVec v ∧ v ⬝ᵥ A *ᵥ v = 0\n33\t\n34\t/-- The **weak** [HM] hypothesis (the natural Hasse–Minkowski output): every indefinite even unimodular form\n35\thas a *nonzero* (not necessarily primitive) integer isotropic vector. -/\n36\tabbrev HasWeakIsotropicVectorHyp : Prop :=\n37\t  ∀ {m : ℕ} (A : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) ℤ), IsEvenUnimodular A →\n38\t    0 < sigPos (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap' →\n39\t    0 < sigNeg (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap' →\n40\t    ∃ v : Fin m → ℤ, v ≠ 0 ∧ v ⬝ᵥ A *ᵥ v = 0\n41\t\n42\t/-- **Weak ⟹ strong [HM].** The Hasse–Minkowski output (a nonzero integer isotropic vector) is upgraded to a\n43\t*primitive* one by content extraction (`exists_primitive_isotropic_of_isotropic`). So discharging the weak\n44\thypothesis (the actual goal of the Hilbert-symbol / Hasse–Minkowski build) immediately discharges the strong\n45\tform consumed by the capstone — and hence the whole `16 ∣ σ` chain. -/\n46\ttheorem hasIsotropic_of_weak (hw : HasWeakIsotropicVectorHyp) : HasIsotropicVectorHyp := by\n47\t  intro m A hA hsp hsn\n48\t  obtain ⟨v, hv0, hviso⟩ := hw A hA hsp hsn\n49\t  exact exists_primitive_isotropic_of_isotropic A v hv0 hviso\n50\t\n51\t/-- **`8 ∣ σ` for even unimodular forms, with [Θ] discharged.** Given only the [HM] input, every even\n52\tunimodular integer form has `8 ∣ latticeSig`. The definite branch is supplied unconditionally by\n53\ttheta-modularity (`eight_dvd_latticeSig_of_definite`). -/\n54\ttheorem eight_dvd_latticeSig_of_HM (hHM : HasIsotropicVectorHyp)\n55\t    (n : ℕ) (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ) (heu : IsEvenUnimodular M) : 8 ∣ latticeSig M :=\n56\t  eight_dvd_latticeSig_of_HM_of_Theta hHM (fun A => eight_dvd_latticeSig_of_definite A) n M heu\n57\t\n58\t/-- **`16 ∣ σ` for even unimodular forms, with [Θ] discharged.** Given the [HM] input and the topological\n59\tfactor `2 ∣ σ/8`, every even unimodular form has `16 ∣ latticeSig`. -/\n60\ttheorem sixteen_dvd_latticeSig_of_HM_of_topo {n : ℕ} (hHM : HasIsotropicVectorHyp)\n61\t    (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ) (heu : IsEvenUnimodular M) (htopo : (2 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSig M / 8) :\n62\t    (16 : ℤ) ∣ latticeSig M :=\n63\t  sixteen_dvd_latticeSig_of_HM_of_Theta_of_topo hHM (fun A => eight_dvd_latticeSig_of_definite A) M heu htopo\n64\t\n65\tend SKEFTHawking\n66\t"}]},"uuid":"f9d78042-7616-4bd6-bc29-205a29a62da5","timestamp":"2026-08-15T19:38:49.558Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"dfd36d3d-2746-41f7-b340-de43a2a4a552","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/johnroehm/Programming/PythonEnvironments/Physics/Fluid-Based-Physics-Research/SK_EFT_Hawking","sessionId":"6cf37aa2-f1c7-4e33-a712-f17c2d75d99b","version":"2.1.229","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"swift-singing-llama"}

{"parentUuid":"1033b960-c4de-4679-9388-f9f0c4cd60c1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d7b89794-41f0-46fe-9a20-6d198f8f624f","agentId":"a5ea667e8aa34be02","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015jUosNmt7iepEB1R4VHwGg","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/-\n2\tPhase 5q.B: van der Blij `8 ∣ σ` reduced to its two classical inputs.\n3\t\n4\tThis is the capstone of the classification route to van der Blij's theorem. It assembles the entire\n5\tsignature calculus and the hyperbolic-plane split-off recursion into a single reduction:\n6\t\n7\t  **`eight_dvd_latticeSig_of_HM_of_Theta`** — for *every* even unimodular integer form `M`,\n8\t  `8 ∣ latticeSig M`, GIVEN only the two precisely-isolated classical inputs:\n9\t   • **[HM]**  an indefinite (`sigPos > 0 ∧ sigNeg > 0`) even unimodular form has a *primitive isotropic\n10\t     vector* (Hasse–Minkowski / Meyer), and\n11\t   • **[Θ]**  a definite (`sigPos = 0 ∨ sigNeg = 0`) even unimodular form has `8 ∣ σ` (theta-modularity:\n12\t     `8 ∣ rank`).\n13\t\n14\tEverything between these two inputs and the conclusion is now machine-checked kernel-pure: the generator\n15\tsignatures (`σ(E₈)=8`, `σ(H)=0`), Sylvester congruence-invariance, block additivity, the primitive-vector /\n16\thyperbolic-pair construction, the split-off-H step `latticeSig M = latticeSigOf (residGram)`\n17\t(`SplitHyperbolic.latticeSig_split`), and the fact that the residual is again even unimodular of rank `n−2`\n18\t(`residGram_evenUnimodular`). The proof is strong induction on the rank: definite forms hit [Θ]; indefinite\n19\tforms peel a hyperbolic plane via [HM] and recurse.\n20\t\n21\tThis isolates the *only* remaining content of van der Blij to two named classical theorems (neither yet in\n22\tMathlib): the local-global Hasse–Minkowski input and the theta-modularity input. The dependency graph is\n23\tclean and anti-circular: it routes `even-unimodular ⇒ 8∣σ` through [HM]+[Θ], never through ABP / Rokhlin.\n24\t\n25\tAll proofs are kernel-pure (`propext`/`Classical.choice`/`Quot.sound` only); no `native_decide`, no\n26\t`maxHeartbeats`, no axiom (the two classical inputs are explicit *hypotheses*, not axioms).\n27\t-/\n28\t\n29\timport Mathlib\n30\timport SKEFTHawking.AlgebraicRokhlin\n31\timport SKEFTHawking.LatticePrimitive\n32\timport SKEFTHawking.LatticeSignature\n33\timport SKEFTHawking.LatticeSigBlock\n34\timport SKEFTHawking.SplitHyperbolic\n35\timport SKEFTHawking.RokhlinClassification\n36\t\n37\tnamespace SKEFTHawking\n38\t\n39\topen Matrix Module QuadraticForm\n40\t\n41\t/-- **van der Blij, reduced to [HM] + [Θ].** Given that every *indefinite* even unimodular form has a\n42\tprimitive isotropic vector ([HM]) and every *definite* even unimodular form has `8 ∣ σ` ([Θ]), every even\n43\tunimodular integer form has `8 ∣ latticeSig`. Proof: strong induction on rank — definite forms use [Θ]\n44\tdirectly; indefinite forms produce a hyperbolic pair ([HM] + `exists_hyperbolic_pair`), split off a\n45\thyperbolic plane (`latticeSig_split`: `latticeSig M = latticeSigOf (residGram)`) leaving an even unimodular\n46\tresidual of rank `n−2` (`residGram_evenUnimodular`), and recurse. -/\n47\ttheorem eight_dvd_latticeSig_of_HM_of_Theta\n48\t    (hHM : ∀ {m : ℕ} (A : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) ℤ), IsEvenUnimodular A →\n49\t      0 < sigPos (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap' →\n50\t      0 < sigNeg (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap' →\n51\t      ∃ v : Fin m → ℤ, IsPrimitiveVec v ∧ v ⬝ᵥ A *ᵥ v = 0)\n52\t    (hΘ : ∀ {m : ℕ} (A : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) ℤ), IsEvenUnimodular A →\n53\t      (sigPos (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap' = 0 ∨\n54\t       sigNeg (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap' = 0) → 8 ∣ latticeSig A) :\n55\t    ∀ (n : ℕ) (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ), {"parentUuid":"605b6e31-6514-43a1-b4e2-1c087df3f9ff","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d7b89794-41f0-46fe-9a20-6d198f8f624f","agentId":"a5ea667e8aa34be02","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JVcNY2dAMHstSqzocN3e74","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/-\n2\tPhase 5q.B, [Θ4]: the modular-weight finish — an even unimodular lattice has rank `8 ∣ d`.\n3\t\n4\tThe theta S-transformation (`ThetaSTransform.latticeTheta_S`) plus the T-invariance\n5\t(`LatticeTheta.latticeTheta_T_int`) make `Θ_G` a nonzero level-1 modular object of weight `d/2`; the automorphy\n6\tmultiplier `(τ/i)^{d/2}` is consistent under `SL₂(ℤ)` (the relation `S² = (ST)³`) only when `8 ∣ d`.\n7\t\n8\tThis module builds the bricks: the cast/inverse commutation `(A.map ℤ→ℝ)⁻¹ = A⁻¹.map ℤ→ℝ`, the theta\n9\tself-duality `Θ_{M⁻¹} = Θ_M` for an even unimodular `M` (via `latticeTheta_congr` with `P = A⁻¹`), and the\n10\tresulting self-transform `Θ_M(-1/τ) = (τ/i)^{d/2} Θ_M(τ)`.\n11\t\n12\tAll proofs are kernel-pure (`propext`/`Classical.choice`/`Quot.sound` only); no `native_decide`, no\n13\t`maxHeartbeats`, no axiom.\n14\t-/\n15\t\n16\timport Mathlib\n17\timport SKEFTHawking.ThetaSTransform\n18\timport SKEFTHawking.ThetaModularity\n19\t\n20\tnamespace SKEFTHawking\n21\t\n22\topen Matrix Complex\n23\topen scoped Real\n24\t\n25\t/-- The integer-cast of a matrix commutes with the (nonsingular) inverse, for a matrix with unit determinant:\n26\t`(A.map ℤ→ℝ)⁻¹ = A⁻¹.map ℤ→ℝ`. (Both inverses are left-inverses of `A.map ℤ→ℝ`, since `A⁻¹·A = 1` over `ℤ`\n27\tcasts entrywise.) -/\n28\ttheorem cast_map_inv {d : ℕ} (A : Matrix (Fin d) (Fin d) ℤ) (hunit : IsUnit A.det) :\n29\t    (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ))⁻¹ = A⁻¹.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ) := by\n30\t  apply Matrix.inv_eq_left_inv\n31\t  ext i j\n32\t  simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.map_apply, ← Int.cast_mul, ← Int.cast_sum]\n33\t  rw [show (∑ k, A⁻¹ i k * A k j) = (A⁻¹ * A) i j from (Matrix.mul_apply).symm,\n34\t    Matrix.nonsing_inv_mul A hunit]\n35\t  simp [Matrix.one_apply, apply_ite (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)]\n36\t\n37\t/-- **Theta self-duality** for an even unimodular integer form `A` (`Aᵀ = A`, `det A` a unit): with `M = A.map\n38\tℤ→ℝ`, `Θ_{M⁻¹} = Θ_M`. The dual lattice `M⁻¹ = (A⁻¹)ᵀ·M·(A⁻¹)` is `M` reindexed by the unimodular `A⁻¹`, so\n39\t`latticeTheta_congr` (with `P = A⁻¹`) identifies their thetas. -/\n40\ttheorem latticeTheta_inv_eq {d : ℕ} (A : Matrix (Fin d) (Fin d) ℤ) (hsymm : Aᵀ = A)\n41\t    (hunit : IsUnit A.det) (τ : ℂ) :\n42\t    latticeTheta ((A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ))⁻¹) τ = latticeTheta (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)) τ := by\n43\t  have hPdet : IsUnit (A⁻¹).det := by\n44\t    rw [Matrix.det_nonsing_inv]; exact hunit.ringInverse\n45\t  have hAinvT : (A⁻¹)ᵀ = A⁻¹ := by rw [Matrix.transpose_nonsing_inv, hsymm]\n46\t  have hLI : (A⁻¹).map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ) * A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ) = 1 := by\n47\t    ext i j\n48\t    simp only [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.map_apply, ← Int.cast_mul, ← Int.cast_sum]\n49\t    rw [show (∑ k, A⁻¹ i k * A k j) = (A⁻¹ * A) i j from (Matrix.mul_apply).symm,\n50\t      Matrix.nonsing_inv_mul A hunit]\n51\t    simp [Matrix.one_apply, apply_ite (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)]\n52\t  rw [cast_map_inv A hunit, ← latticeTheta_congr A⁻¹ hPdet (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)) τ]\n53\t  congr 1\n54\t  rw [← Matrix.transpose_map, hAinvT, hLI, Matrix.one_mul]\n55\t\n56\t/-- **The theta S self-transformation** for an even unimodular integer form `A` (`Aᵀ = A`, positive-definite\n57\tcast, hence `det A = 1`): with `M = A.map ℤ→ℝ` and `Im τ > 0`,\n58\t> `Θ_M(-1/τ) = (τ/i)^{d/2} · Θ_M(τ)`.\n59\tFrom `latticeTheta_S` (`det M = 1` kills the `(det)^{-1/2}`; `π/(-iπσ) = i/σ`), `latticeTheta_inv_eq`\n60\t(`Θ_{M⁻¹} = Θ_M`), and the substitution `σ = -1/τ`. The automorphy multiplier whose `SL₂(ℤ)`-consistency\n61\tforces `8 ∣ d`. -/\n62\ttheorem latticeTheta_S_self {d : ℕ} (A : Matrix (Fin d) (Fin d) ℤ) (hsymm : Aᵀ = A)\n63\t    (hunim : IsUnimodular A) (hpd : (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).PosDef) {τ : ℂ} (hτ : 0 < τ.im) :\n64\t    latticeTheta (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)) (-1 / τ)\n65\t      = (τ / I) ^ ((d : ℂ) / 2) * latticeTheta (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)) τ := by\n66\t  have hAdet : A.det = 1 := posDef_unimodular_det_one A hunim hpd\n67\t  have hunit : IsUnit A.det := by rw [hAdet]; exact isUnit_one\n68\t  have hdet1 : (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).det = 1 := by rw [← Int.cast_det, hAdet, Int.cast_one]\n69\t  have hτ0 : τ ≠ 0 := fun h => by rw [h] at hτ; simp at hτ\n70\t  have hπ : (π : ℂ) ≠ 0 := Complex.ofReal_ne_zero.mpr Real.pi_ne_zero\n71\t  have hσim : 0 < (-1 / τ).im := by\n72\t    have heq : (-1 / τ).im = τ.im / Complex.normSq τ := by\n73\t      rw [neg_div, one_div, Complex.neg_im, Complex.inv_im]; ring\n74\t    rw [heq]; exact div_pos hτ (Complex.normSq_pos.mpr hτ0)\n75\t  have hS := latticeTheta_S hpd hσim\n76\t  rw [hdet1, Real.sqrt_one, Complex.ofReal_one, inv_one, one_mul,\n77\t    latticeTheta_inv_eq A hsymm hunit, show (-1 / (-1 / τ)) = τ from by field_simp] at hS\n78\t  rw [hS]\n79\t  congr 2\n80\t  rw [div_eq_div_iff (by simp [hπ, hτ0, Complex.I_ne_zero]) Complex.I_ne_zero]\n81\t  field_simp\n82\t\n83\t/-- **The S-multiplier consistency**: `(-i)^{d/2} = 1 ⟺ 8 ∣ d`. Since `(-i)^{d/2} = exp(-iπd/4)`, it is `1`\n84\texactly when `8 ∣ d`. This is the arithmetic core of the modular-weight constraint: the `(ST)³`-relation\n85\tforces the theta automorphy factor `(-i)^{d/2}` to be `1`, hence `8 ∣ d`. -/\n86\ttheorem neg_I_cpow_eq_one_iff_eight_dvd {d : ℕ} :\n87\t    (-I) ^ ((d : ℂ) / 2) = 1 ↔ 8 ∣ d := by\n88\t  have hval : (-I) ^ ((d : ℂ) / 2) = Complex.exp (-((π : ℂ) * d / 4) * I) := by\n89\t    rw [Complex.cpow_def_of_ne_zero (by simp), Complex.log_neg_I]; ring_nf\n90\t  rw [hval, Complex.exp_eq_one_iff]\n91\t  constructor\n92\t  · rintro ⟨n, hn⟩\n93\t    have hcancel : -((π : ℂ) * d / 4) = (n : ℂ) * 2 * π := by\n94\t      have h1 : -((π : ℂ) * d / 4) * I = ((n : ℂ) * 2 * π) * I := by rw [hn]; ring\n95\t      exact mul_right_cancel₀ Complex.I_ne_zero h1\n96\t    have hπc : (π : ℂ) ≠ 0 := Complex.ofReal_ne_zero.mpr Real.pi_ne_zero\n97\t    field_simp at hcancel\n98\t    have hdℤ : (d : ℤ) = -8 * n := by\n99\t      have hc : (d : ℂ) = -8 * (n : ℂ) := by linear_combination -hcancel\n100\t      exact_mod_cast hc\n101\t    omega\n102\t  · rintro ⟨k, rfl⟩\n103\t    exact ⟨-(k : ℤ), by push_cast; ring⟩\n104\t\n105\t/-- The combined `ST`-step of the theta automorphy: `θ_M(-1/(w+1)) = ((w+1)/i)^{d/2}·θ_M(w)` for `w ∈ UHP`.\n106\t(S self-transform at `w+1 ∈ UHP`, then `T`-periodicity `θ_M(w+1) = θ_M(w)`.) Iterating this three times around\n107\tthe relation `(ST)³ = 1` yields the `8 ∣ d` constraint. -/\n108\ttheorem theta_ST {d : ℕ} (A : Matrix (Fin d) (Fin d) ℤ) (hsymm : Aᵀ = A) (hunim : IsUnimodular A)\n109\t    (hpd : (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).PosDef) (heven : ∀ i, 2 ∣ A i i) {w : ℂ} (hw : 0 < w.im) :\n110\t    latticeTheta (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)) (-1 / (w + 1))\n111\t      = ((w + 1) / I) ^ ((d : ℂ) / 2) * latticeTheta (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)) w := by\n112\t  have hw1 : 0 < (w + 1).im := by simpa using hw\n113\t  rw [latticeTheta_S_self A hsymm hunim hpd hw1, latticeTheta_T_int A hsymm heven]\n114\t\n115\t/-- **Complex-power base multiplicativity under the principal-branch arg condition**:\n116\t`(x·y)^s = x^s · y^s` when `arg x + arg y ∈ (-π, π]` (and `x, y ≠ 0`). From `log(x·y) = log x + log y`\n117\t(`Complex.log_mul_eq_add_log_iff`) and `cpow_def`. The tool for combining the three `(·/i)^{d/2}` automorphy\n118\tfactors of the `(ST)³` relation. -/\n119\ttheorem mul_cpow_of_arg_mem {x y : ℂ} (s : ℂ) (hx : x ≠ 0) (hy : y ≠ 0)\n120\t    (harg : x.arg + y.arg ∈ Set.Ioc (-π) π) :\n121\t    (x * y) ^ s = x ^ s * y ^ s := by\n122\t  rw [Complex.cpow_def_of_ne_zero (mul_ne_zero hx hy), Complex.cpow_def_of_ne_zero hx,\n123\t    Complex.cpow_def_of_ne_zero hy, ← Complex.exp_add, ← add_mul,\n124\t    (Complex.log_mul_eq_add_log_iff hx hy).mpr harg]\n125\t\n126\t/-- `Θ_M(i) ≠ 0` for positive-definite `M`: at `τ = i` the lattice theta is the sum of the *positive reals*\n127\t`exp(-π·vᵀMv)` (real part `≥ 1` from the `v = 0` term), hence nonzero. Provides the `θ(τ) ≠ 0` needed to divide\n128\tout of the `(ST)³` automorphy identity. -/\n129\ttheorem latticeTheta_I_ne_zero {d : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin d) (Fin d) ℝ) (hpd : M.PosDef) :\n130\t    latticeTheta M I ≠ 0 := by\n131\t  have hsummand : ∀ v : Fin d → ℤ,\n132\t      Complex.exp (π * I * I * (((fun i => (v i : ℝ)) ⬝ᵥ M *ᵥ (fun i => (v i : ℝ)) : ℝ) : ℂ))\n133\t        = Complex.ofReal (Real.exp (-(π * ((fun i => (v i : ℝ)) ⬝ᵥ M *ᵥ (fun i => (v i : ℝ)))))) := by\n134\t    intro v\n135\t    rw [mul_assoc (π : ℂ) I I, Complex.I_mul_I, Complex.ofReal_exp]\n136\t    congr 1\n137\t    push_cast; ring\n138\t  have hsumR : Summable (fun v : Fin d → ℤ =>\n139\t      Real.exp (-(π * ((fun i => (v i : ℝ)) ⬝ᵥ M *ᵥ (fun i => (v i : ℝ)))))) :=\n140\t    Complex.summable_ofReal.mp ((summable_gram_gaussian M hpd I (by simp)).congr hsummand)\n141\t  have hL : latticeTheta M I\n142\t      = Complex.ofReal (∑' v : Fin d → ℤ,\n143\t          Real.exp (-(π * ((fun i => (v i : ℝ)) ⬝ᵥ M *ᵥ (fun i => (v i : ℝ)))))) := by\n144\t    rw [latticeTheta, Complex.ofReal_tsum]\n145\t    exact tsum_congr hsummand\n146\t  rw [hL, ne_eq, Complex.ofReal_eq_zero]\n147\t  exact ne_of_gt (hsumR.tsum_pos (fun v => (Real.exp_pos _).le) 0 (Real.exp_pos _))\n148\t\n149\t/-- The upper half-plane is preserved by `τ ↦ -1/τ`: `0 < τ.im → 0 < (-1/τ).im`\n150\t(`(-1/τ).im = τ.im / |τ|²`). -/\n151\ttheorem neg_inv_im_pos {τ : ℂ} (hτ : 0 < τ.im) : 0 < (-1 / τ).im := by\n152\t  have hτ0 : τ ≠ 0 := fun h => by rw [h] at hτ; simp at hτ\n153\t  have heq : (-1 / τ).im = τ.im / Complex.normSq τ := by\n154\t    rw [neg_div, one_div, Complex.neg_im, Complex.inv_im]; ring\n155\t  rw [heq]; exact div_pos hτ (Complex.normSq_pos.mpr hτ0)\n156\t\n157\t/-- Right-half-plane specialization of `mul_cpow_of_arg_mem`: if `0 < x.re` and `0 < y.re` then\n158\t`(x·y)^s = x^s · y^s` (both args lie in `(-π/2, π/2)`, so their sum is in `(-π, π]`). -/\n159\ttheorem mul_cpow_of_re_pos {x y : ℂ} (s : ℂ) (hx : 0 < x.re) (hy : 0 < y.re) :\n160\t    (x * y) ^ s = x ^ s * y ^ s := by\n161\t  refine mul_cpow_of_arg_mem s (fun h => by simp [h] at hx) (fun h => by simp [h] at hy) ?_\n162\t  rw [Set.mem_Ioc]\n163\t  refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩\n164\t  · have h1 := Complex.neg_pi_div_two_lt_arg_iff.mpr (Or.inl hx)\n165\t    have h2 := Complex.neg_pi_div_two_lt_arg_iff.mpr (Or.inl hy)\n166\t    linarith\n167\t  · have h1 := Complex.arg_le_pi_div_two_iff.mpr (Or.inl hx.le)\n168\t    have h2 := Complex.arg_le_pi_div_two_iff.mpr (Or.inl hy.le)\n169\t    linarith\n170\t\n171\t/-- **[Θ4] — the rank of a positive-definite even unimodular lattice is divisible by 8.** For an even\n172\tunimodular integer form `A` (`Aᵀ = A`, even diagonal, positive-definite cast), `8 ∣ d`. The theta function\n173\t`Θ_M` satisfies the `S` self-transform `Θ_M(-1/τ) = (τ/i)^{d/2}Θ_M(τ)` and `T`-periodicity; iterating the\n174\tcombined `ST`-step three times around `(ST)³ = 1` (with `Θ_M(i) ≠ 0`) forces the automorphy factor\n175\t`(-i)^{d/2} = 1`, i.e. `8 ∣ d`. -/\n176\ttheorem eight_dvd_rank {d : ℕ} (A : Matrix (Fin d) (Fin d) ℤ) (hsymm : Aᵀ = A)\n177\t    (hunim : IsUnimodular A) (hpd : (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).PosDef) (heven : ∀ i, 2 ∣ A i i) :\n178\t    8 ∣ d := by\n179\t  rw [← neg_I_cpow_eq_one_iff_eight_dvd]\n180\t  have hIne : (I : ℂ) ≠ 0 := Complex.I_ne_zero\n181\t  have hI1ne : (I + 1 : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by\n182\t    intro h; have := congrArg Complex.im h; simp at this\n183\t  have hIim : (0 : ℝ) < I.im := by simp\n184\t  have hp1im : (0 : ℝ) < ((I - 1) / 2).im := by simp\n185\t  have hp2im : (0 : ℝ) < (I - 1).im := by simp\n186\t  have m1 : (-1 / (I + 1) : ℂ) = (I - 1) / 2 := by field_simp; linear_combination -Complex.I_sq\n187\t  have m2 : (-1 / ((I - 1) / 2 + 1) : ℂ) = I - 1 := by\n188\t    rw [show ((I - 1) / 2 + 1 : ℂ) = (I + 1) / 2 from by ring]\n189\t    field_simp; linear_combination -Complex.I_sq\n190\t  have m3 : (-1 / ((I - 1) + 1) : ℂ) = I := by field_simp; linear_combination -Complex.I_sq\n191\t  have e1 := theta_ST A hsymm hunim hpd heven hIim\n192\t  rw [m1] at e1\n193\t  have e2 := theta_ST A hsymm hunim hpd heven hp1im\n194\t  rw [m2] at e2\n195\t  have e3 := theta_ST A hsymm hunim hpd heven hp2im\n196\t  rw [m3, e2, e1] at e3\n197\t  have hθ := latticeTheta_I_ne_zero (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)) hpd\n198\t  have hK : (1 : ℂ) = ((I - 1 + 1) / I) ^ ((d : ℂ) / 2)\n199\t      * (((I - 1) / 2 + 1) / I) ^ ((d : ℂ) / 2) * ((I + 1) / I) ^ ((d : ℂ) / 2) := by\n200\t    refine mul_right_cancel₀ hθ ?_\n201\t    rw [one_mul]; nth_rewrite 1 [e3]; ring\n202\t  have hCre : (0 : ℝ) < ((I + 1) / I).re := by\n203\t    rw [show (I + 1) / I = 1 - I from by field_simp; linear_combination Complex.I_sq]; simp\n204\t  have hBre : (0 : ℝ) < (((I - 1) / 2 + 1) / I).re := by\n205\t    rw [show ((I - 1) / 2 + 1) / I = (1 - I) / 2 from by field_simp; linear_combination Complex.I_sq]\n206\t    simp\n207\t  rw [show (I - 1 + 1) / I = 1 from by rw [show I - 1 + 1 = I from by ring]; exact div_self hIne,\n208\t    Complex.one_cpow, one_mul, ← mul_cpow_of_re_pos ((d : ℂ) / 2) hBre hCre,\n209\t    show ((I - 1) / 2 + 1) / I * ((I + 1) / I) = -I from by\n210\t      field_simp; linear_combination (2 * I + 1) * Complex.I_sq]\n211\t    at hK\n212\t  exact hK.symm\n213\t\n214\tend SKEFTHawking\n215\t"}]},"uuid":"f78ff86a-22cc-43a2-a4d6-434a623148a0","timestamp":"2026-08-15T19:39:10.269Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"605b6e31-6514-43a1-b4e2-1c087df3f9ff","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/johnroehm/Programming/PythonEnvironments/Physics/Fluid-Based-Physics-Research/SK_EFT_Hawking","sessionId":"6cf37aa2-f1c7-4e33-a712-f17c2d75d99b","version":"2.1.229","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"swift-singing-llama"}

{"parentUuid":"cf012345-0c5b-4c93-ae32-798d0d914b67","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d7b89794-41f0-46fe-9a20-6d198f8f624f","agentId":"a5ea667e8aa34be02","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Gbw9s96P3EY1T6q87J3nhP","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/-\n2\t# NO-GO: the lattice Arf invariant does NOT compute σ/8 mod 2\n3\t\n4\t**The mod-16 factor of Rokhlin's theorem is irreducibly GEOMETRIC, not a lattice invariant.**\n5\t\n6\tThis module records, as kernel-pure proved theorems, the refutation of a tempting-but-FALSE bridge that\n7\twas carried as prose across two planning sessions (Phase 5q.B/5q.C) before being computed and refuted\n8\t(2026-06-13). The false claim, propagated from an internally-contradictory deep-research note\n9\t(`Lit-Search/Phase-5c/Rokhlin/…`), was:\n10\t\n11\t  **(FALSE)**  `σ(L)/8 ≡ Arf(q̄) (mod 2)` for every even unimodular lattice `L`, with `q̄ = redQuad`\n12\t                the mod-2 quadratic refinement `q̄(x)=(x·x)/2 mod 2` on `L/2L`.\n13\t\n14\tIt is FALSE, refuted by `E₈`:\n15\t  • `σ(E₈) = 8` (E₈ positive definite), so `σ/8 = 1` (ODD); but\n16\t  • `gaussSum (redQuad E₈) = +16 = +2⁴` (`#{q̄=0}=136=2⁷+2³`, the `Arf=0` fingerprint; the 120 classes with\n17\t    `q̄=1` are exactly E₈'s 240 roots / ±pairs) ⟹ **`Arf(redQuad E₈) = 0`** (`arfOfForm E₈ = 0`).\n18\tSo the bridge would read `1 ≡ 0 (mod 2)` — false (`lattice_arf_bridge_refuted`).\n19\t\n20\t## Why it can never be fixed in the lattice category (the structural no-go)\n21\t\n22\t1. **`arfOfForm ≡ 0` on every even unimodular lattice.** Each generator has Arf 0\n23\t   (`arfOfForm_e8lit_eq_zero`, `arfOfForm_hyp_eq_zero`, `arfOfForm_neg_e8lit_eq_zero`), and `arfOfForm`\n24\t   is additive over `⊕` (gaussSum multiplicative, all factors on the `+2^{gᵢ}` branch). So it is a\n25\t   CONSTANT (0), while `σ/8` varies — they cannot be equal.\n26\t2. **Orientation-blindness (`redQuad_neg_eq`/`arfOfForm_neg_eq`).** `redQuad(−M) = redQuad(M)` (every\n27\t   coefficient is fixed mod 2 under negation), so `arfOfForm(−M) = arfOfForm(M)`; but\n28\t   `latticeSig(−M) = −latticeSig(M)`. No `redQuad`-derived invariant can track the *sign* of `σ`.\n29\t3. **Discriminant-form ceiling.** For a *unimodular* lattice the discriminant group `L*/L` is trivial,\n30\t   so the entire finite-quadratic-form (Milgram/Brown/Arf) apparatus can certify only `σ ≡ 0 mod 8`\n31\t   (van der Blij — already proven in-repo as `eight_dvd_latticeSig`) and is BLIND to `σ mod 16`. (The\n32\t   ℤ/4 Brown refinement `(x·x) mod 4` takes only even values on an even lattice, collapsing to the same\n33\t   ±1 Arf sum.)\n34\t\n35\t## What IS true (the correct statements)\n36\t\n37\t- **van der Blij** is a *mod-8* theorem (`c² ≡ σ mod 8` via characteristic vectors; `0` is characteristic\n38\t  for even lattices ⟹ `8∣σ`). The project has this: `SKEFTHawking.eight_dvd_latticeSig`.\n39\t- The genuine `σ/8 ≡ Arf` relation is the **GEOMETRIC** Freedman–Kirby / Guillou–Marin congruence\n40\t  `σ(M) ≡ Σ·Σ + 8·Arf(M,Σ) mod 16`, where `Arf(M,Σ)` is the Arf invariant of the quadratic refinement on\n41\t  `H₁(Σ;ℤ/2)` of a smoothly embedded *characteristic surface* `Σ` in a spin 4-MANIFOLD — data not present\n42\t  in, and not recoverable from, the lattice. (`E₈`'s *characteristic-surface* Arf is `1`; its *lattice*\n43\t  L/2L Arf is `0` — these are different invariants, and conflating them is the original error.)\n44\t- Hence `16∣σ` for smooth spin 4-manifolds carries exactly ONE irreducible topological input, by\n45\t  mathematical necessity (E₈ is the even-unimodular counterexample with σ=8). The project carries it as\n46\t  the tracked hypothesis `SmoothSpinManifold4.topo : 2∣σ/8` (ADR-003). It CANNOT be re-expressed as a\n47\t  lattice-algebraic `Arf=0`; the only honest `Arf=0` form is the geometric one (ADR-003 Phase-2 frontier).\n48\t\n49\tThe `redQuad`/`gaussSum` machinery in `EvenLatticeForm.lean`/`ArfInvariant.lean` is itself correct and\n50\treusable — it correctly proves `gaussSum(redQuad M) = 2^g·(−1)^{Arf}` and `arfOfForm ≡ 0`; it simply does\n51\tNOT drive a `σ mod 16` bridge.\n52\t\n53\tSee: ADR-003 (irreducible-topological-residue posture), `docs/roadmaps/Phase5qC_ArfBridge_Roadmap.md`\n54\t(retired premise), `docs/audits/` no-go register. Kernel-pure (`propext`/`Classical.choice`/`Quot.sound`\n55\tonly); no `native_decide`, no `sorry`, no `maxHeartbeats`.\n56\t-/\n57\t\n58\timport Mathlib\n59\timport SKEFTHawking.ArfInvariant\n60\timport SKEFTHawking.EvenLatticeForm\n61\timport SKEFTHawking.E8Literal\n62\timport SKEFTHawking.E8Signature\n63\timport SKEFTHawking.LatticeSignature\n64\timport SKEFTHawking.GeneratorNondeg\n65\t\n66\tnamespace SKEFTHawking.RokhlinArfNoGo\n67\t\n68\topen SKEFTHawking SKEFTHawking.EvenLattice Matrix\n69\t\n70\t/-- The candidate **algebraic Arf invariant** of an even unimodular lattice: the Arf invariant of the\n71\tmod-2 quadratic refinement `redQuad M`, read off the sign of its genus-`g` Gauss sum\n72\t(`gaussSum (redQuad M) = 2^g·(−1)^{Arf}`, so a positive Gauss sum ↔ `Arf = 0`). This is the exact object\n73\tthe (false) Phase-5q.C roadmap bridge proposed as a replacement for the tracked topological input\n74\t`SmoothSpinManifold4.topo : 2 ∣ σ/8`. It is well-defined and kernel-pure, but — as the theorems below\n75\tshow — it is **identically `0`** on even unimodular lattices and does NOT equal `σ/8 mod 2`. -/\n76\tnoncomputable def arfOfForm {n : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ) : ZMod 2 :=\n77\t  if 0 < Arf.gaussSum (redQuad M) then 0 else 1\n78\t\n79\tset_option maxRecDepth 4000 in\n80\t/-- **Witness (kernel `decide`, `native_decide`-free):** `gaussSum (redQuad E₈) = +16 = +2⁴`, the\n81\t`Arf = 0` branch. (Independent cross-check: `#{x : redQuad E₈ x = 0} = 136 = 2⁷+2³`, and the 120 classes\n82\twith `q̄ = 1` are exactly E₈'s 240 roots in ±pairs.) -/\n83\ttheorem gaussSum_redQuad_e8lit_eq : Arf.gaussSum (redQuad E8lit) = 16 := by decide\n84\t\n85\t/-- **`arfOfForm E₈ = 0`** — NOT `1`, contradicting the roadmap's Wave-C.2 assertion `arfOfForm E₈ = 1`. -/\n86\ttheorem arfOfForm_e8lit_eq_zero : arfOfForm E8lit = 0 := by\n87\t  unfold arfOfForm; rw [gaussSum_redQuad_e8lit_eq]; norm_num\n88\t\n89\t/-- `gaussSum (redQuad H) = +2 = +2¹` (the hyperbolic plane, `Arf 0`). -/\n90\ttheorem gaussSum_redQuad_hyp_eq : Arf.gaussSum (redQuad Hyp) = 2 := by decide\n91\t\n92\t/-- **`arfOfForm H = 0`.** -/\n93\ttheorem arfOfForm_hyp_eq_zero : arfOfForm Hyp = 0 := by\n94\t  unfold arfOfForm; rw [gaussSum_redQuad_hyp_eq]; norm_num\n95\t\n96\t/-- **Orientation-blindness of `redQuad`:** `redQuad(−M) = redQuad(M)` for even `M`. Every coefficient of\n97\t`redQuad` is fixed mod 2 under negation (`-a ≡ a (mod 2)`, and `-(2k)/2 = -k ≡ k (mod 2)` for the even\n98\tdiagonal). This is the structural reason no `redQuad`-derived invariant can see the *sign* of the\n99\tsignature. -/\n100\ttheorem redQuad_neg_eq {n : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ) (heven : ∀ i, 2 ∣ M i i) :\n101\t    redQuad (-M) = redQuad M := by\n102\t  funext x\n103\t  unfold redQuad hdSum upperSum\n104\t  congr 1\n105\t  · refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_\n106\t    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := heven i\n107\t    simp only [Matrix.neg_apply, hk]\n108\t    rw [show (-(2 * k)) / 2 = -k from by omega, show (2 * k) / 2 = k from by omega,\n109\t      Int.cast_neg, CharTwo.neg_eq]\n110\t  · refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_\n111\t    simp only [Matrix.neg_apply]\n112\t    by_cases h : i < j <;> simp only [h, if_true, if_false, Int.cast_neg, CharTwo.neg_eq]\n113\t\n114\t/-- **`arfOfForm(−M) = arfOfForm(M)`** for even `M` — `arfOfForm` is orientation-blind, whereas\n115\t`latticeSig(−M) = −latticeSig(M)` (`latticeSig_neg`). -/\n116\ttheorem arfOfForm_neg_eq {n : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ) (heven : ∀ i, 2 ∣ M i i) :\n117\t    arfOfForm (-M) = arfOfForm M := by\n118\t  unfold arfOfForm; rw [redQuad_neg_eq M heven]\n119\t\n120\t/-- **`arfOfForm(−E₈) = 0`** (= `arfOfForm E₈`), while `σ(−E₈) = −8 ≠ 8 = σ(E₈)`: a concrete instance of\n121\tthe orientation-blindness obstruction. -/\n122\ttheorem arfOfForm_neg_e8lit_eq_zero : arfOfForm (-E8lit) = 0 := by\n123\t  rw [arfOfForm_neg_eq E8lit e8lit_even, arfOfForm_e8lit_eq_zero]\n124\t\n125\t/-- **THE NO-GO.** The proposed bridge `σ/8 ≡ arfOfForm M (mod 2)` for even unimodular `M` is FALSE,\n126\trefuted by `E₈`: an even unimodular lattice with `σ/8 = 1` (odd) but `arfOfForm = 0`. Equivalently, the\n127\tmod-16 factor of Rokhlin's theorem is irreducibly geometric and cannot be carried by any lattice Arf\n128\tinvariant (the lattice `L/2L` Arf is identically `0`). The honest carrier remains\n129\t`SmoothSpinManifold4.topo : 2 ∣ σ/8` (ADR-003); van der Blij gives only `8 ∣ σ`\n130\t(`SKEFTHawking.eight_dvd_latticeSig`). -/\n131\ttheorem lattice_arf_bridge_refuted :\n132\t    ¬ (∀ {n : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ), IsEvenUnimodular M →\n133\t        ((latticeSig M / 8 : ℤ) : ZMod 2) = arfOfForm M) := by\n134\t  intro h\n135\t  have hE8 := h E8lit e8lit_even_unimodular\n136\t  rw [e8lit_latticeSig, arfOfForm_e8lit_eq_zero] at hE8\n137\t  norm_num at hE8\n138\t\n139\tend SKEFTHawking.RokhlinArfNoGo\n140\t"}]},"uuid":"8826f6ef-6c27-42fa-a958-e706a53e8812","timestamp":"2026-08-15T19:39:21.279Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cf012345-0c5b-4c93-ae32-798d0d914b67","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/johnroehm/Programming/PythonEnvironments/Physics/Fluid-Based-Physics-Research/SK_EFT_Hawking","sessionId":"6cf37aa2-f1c7-4e33-a712-f17c2d75d99b","version":"2.1.229","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"swift-singing-llama"}
{"parentUuid":"e9ec9e2c-80f0-4597-b1fd-04d4f73d426f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d7b89794-41f0-46fe-9a20-6d198f8f624f","agentId":"a5ea667e8aa34be02","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FxvpX8MNcvYe4bGQnXysnc","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/-\n2\tPhase 5q.B Wave B5: the genuine signature of an integer symmetric form, grounded in Mathlib.\n3\t\n4\tUntil now the Rokhlin leg (`AlgebraicRokhlin.lean`, `SpinRokhlinInterface.lean`) carried the\n5\tsignature only as a *free* integer parameter `sigma`/`sig`, unconnected to the form `M`. This module\n6\tcloses that gap: it defines `latticeSig M` as the actual signature `sigPos − sigNeg` of the real\n7\tquadratic form obtained by casting `M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ` to ℝ, using Mathlib's\n8\t`QuadraticMap.sigPos`/`sigNeg` (Sylvester's law of inertia, `Mathlib.LinearAlgebra.QuadraticForm.Signature`).\n9\t\n10\tThis is real infrastructure, not a proxy: `latticeSig` is the topologist's/number-theorist's\n11\tsignature, and the lemmas below are its genuine structural laws — orientation reversal\n12\t`σ(−M) = −σ(M)` and the rank bound `|σ(M)| ≤ n`. All proofs are kernel-pure\n13\t(`propext`/`Classical.choice`/`Quot.sound` only); no `native_decide`, no `maxHeartbeats`, no axiom.\n14\t\n15\tWHAT THIS DOES NOT (YET) DO. It does not prove van der Blij (`IsEvenUnimodular M → 8 ∣ latticeSig M`).\n16\tThat divisibility is the one genuinely arithmetic input remaining on the leg; per the deep-research\n17\tnote (`Lit-Search/Phase-5c/Rokhlin/…`) and a direct Mathlib scout (2026-06-03), it requires either\n18\tthe Hasse–Minkowski classification of indefinite unimodular forms or Milgram–Gauss-sum reciprocity,\n19\tneither of which is in Mathlib (no Witt group, no p-adic quadratic-form theory, no lattice Gauss-sum\n20\treciprocity). The finite Arf/Gauss-sum machinery in `ArfInvariant.lean`/`EvenLatticeForm.lean` is a\n21\tcorrect construction of the mod-2 refinement and its Gauss sum, **but (CORRECTION 2026-06-13) it does NOT\n22\tcapture `σ/8 mod 2`**: the lattice Arf `Arf(redQuad)` is identically `0` on even unimodular forms (E₈:\n23\t`Arf=0` but `σ/8=1`), so the once-claimed `σ/8 mod 2 = Arf(q̄)` is FALSE (`RokhlinArfNoGo.lean`). The mod-16\n24\tfactor `2∣σ/8` is irreducibly geometric (Guillou–Marin / Â-genus-even), carried as `SmoothSpinManifold4.topo`.\n25\tSee the roadmap\n26\t`docs/roadmaps/Phase5qB_SpectraFreeSpinBordism_Roadmap.md` (Wave B1) for the discharge options.\n27\t\n28\tSee also `AlgebraicRokhlin.lean` (the `8 ∣ σ` algebra, conditional on the characteristic-square\n29\tidentity) and `SpinRokhlinInterface.lean` (the wired `16 ∣ σ`).\n30\t-/\n31\t\n32\timport Mathlib\n33\t\n34\tnamespace SKEFTHawking\n35\t\n36\topen QuadraticMap\n37\t\n38\t/-- The **signature** of an integer symmetric form `M`, defined as `sigPos − sigNeg` of the real\n39\tquadratic form obtained by casting `M` to ℝ. This is the genuine (Sylvester-invariant) signature:\n40\tthe number of positive minus the number of negative eigenvalues, counted with multiplicity. -/\n41\tnoncomputable def latticeSig {n : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ) : ℤ :=\n42\t  (sigPos (M.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticForm' : ℤ)\n43\t    - (sigNeg (M.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticForm' : ℤ)\n44\t\n45\t/-- **Orientation reversal:** `σ(−M) = −σ(M)`. Negating the form swaps the positive and negative\n46\tinertia indices, so the signature flips sign. (Topologically: reversing the orientation of a\n47\t4-manifold negates its signature.) -/\n48\ttheorem latticeSig_neg {n : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ) :\n49\t    latticeSig (-M) = - latticeSig M := by\n50\t  have hmap : ((-M).map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)) = -(M.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)) := by\n51\t    ext i j; simp\n52\t  have hqm : ((-M).map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticForm'\n53\t      = -((M.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticForm') := by\n54\t    rw [hmap]; simp [Matrix.toQuadraticForm']\n55\t  unfold latticeSig\n56\t  rw [hqm]\n57\t  show (sigPos (-_) : ℤ) - (sigNeg (-_) : ℤ) = _\n58\t  rw [sigNeg, sigNeg, neg_neg]\n59\t  ring\n60\t\n61\t/-- **Upper rank bound:** `σ(M) ≤ n`. The positive inertia index never exceeds the rank. -/\n62\ttheorem latticeSig_le {n : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ) :\n63\t    latticeSig M ≤ (n : ℤ) := by\n64\t  have h : sigPos (M.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticForm' ≤ n := by\n65\t    simpa using sigPos_le_finrank (M.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticForm'\n66\t  have hneg : (0 : ℤ) ≤ (sigNeg (M.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticForm' : ℤ) := by positivity\n67\t  unfold latticeSig; omega\n68\t\n69\t/-- **Lower rank bound:** `−n ≤ σ(M)`, obtained from the upper bound via orientation reversal. -/\n70\ttheorem latticeSig_ge {n : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ) :\n71\t    -(n : ℤ) ≤ latticeSig M := by\n72\t  have h := latticeSig_le (-M)\n73\t  rw [latticeSig_neg] at h\n74\t  omega\n75\t\n76\t/-- **Signature is bounded by rank:** `|σ(M)| ≤ n`. -/\n77\ttheorem abs_latticeSig_le {n : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ) :\n78\t    |latticeSig M| ≤ (n : ℤ) :=\n79\t  abs_le.mpr ⟨latticeSig_ge M, latticeSig_le M⟩\n80\t\n81\t/-! ## Definite forms: the signature is `±rank`\n82\t\n83\tThese are the `latticeSig = ±n` facts for (positive/negative) definite forms — a stepping stone for the\n84\tfrontier-build sub-wave that handles the *definite* even-unimodular case (where `σ = ±rank`, so `8 ∣ σ ⟺\n85\t8 ∣ rank`). The two `sigPos`/`sigNeg` helpers are general (any finite-dimensional real quadratic form) and\n86\treusable. -/\n87\t\n88\t/-- For a positive-definite quadratic form on a finite-dimensional real space, the positive inertia index is\n89\tthe full dimension. -/\n90\ttheorem sigPos_eq_finrank_of_posDef {M : Type*} [AddCommGroup M] [Module ℝ M]\n91\t    [FiniteDimensional ℝ M] (Q : QuadraticForm ℝ M) (hQ : Q.PosDef) :\n92\t    sigPos Q = Module.finrank ℝ M := by\n93\t  refine le_antisymm (sigPos_le_finrank Q) ?_\n94\t  have hr : (Q.restrict (⊤ : Submodule ℝ M)).PosDef := by\n95\t    intro x hx\n96\t    have hxv : (x : M) ≠ 0 := by rwa [Ne, Submodule.coe_eq_zero]\n97\t    rw [QuadraticMap.restrict_apply]; exact hQ _ hxv\n98\t  simpa using le_sigPos_of_posDef Q hr\n99\t\n100\t/-- For a positive-definite quadratic form, the negative inertia index vanishes. -/\n101\ttheorem sigNeg_eq_zero_of_posDef {M : Type*} [AddCommGroup M] [Module ℝ M]\n102\t    [FiniteDimensional ℝ M] (Q : QuadraticForm ℝ M) (hQ : Q.PosDef) : sigNeg Q = 0 := by\n103\t  rw [sigNeg]\n104\t  obtain ⟨V, hV, hVpos⟩ := exists_finrank_eq_sigPos_and_posDef (-Q)\n105\t  have hVbot : V = ⊥ := by\n106\t    by_contra hne\n107\t    obtain ⟨x, hxV, hx0⟩ := V.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot hne\n108\t    have hpos : (0 : ℝ) < (-Q) x := by\n109\t      have := hVpos ⟨x, hxV⟩ (by simpa [Subtype.ext_iff] using hx0)\n110\t      rwa [QuadraticMap.restrict_apply] at this\n111\t    have hQx : Q x < 0 := by simpa using hpos\n112\t    exact absurd hQx (not_lt.mpr (le_of_lt (hQ x hx0)))\n113\t  rw [← hV, hVbot]; simp\n114\t\n115\t/-- **Positive-definite ⟹ `σ(M) = rank`.** -/\n116\ttheorem latticeSig_of_posDef {n : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ)\n117\t    (hM : (M.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).PosDef) : latticeSig M = (n : ℤ) := by\n118\t  have hQ := hM.toQuadraticForm'\n119\t  unfold latticeSig\n120\t  rw [sigPos_eq_finrank_of_posDef _ hQ, sigNeg_eq_zero_of_posDef _ hQ]\n121\t  simp\n122\t\n123\t/-- **Negative-definite ⟹ `σ(M) = -rank`** (the negation of `M` is positive-definite). -/\n124\ttheorem latticeSig_of_negDef {n : ℕ} (M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℤ)\n125\t    (hM : ((-M).map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).PosDef) : latticeSig M = -(n : ℤ) := by\n126\t  have h := latticeSig_of_posDef (-M) hM\n127\t  rw [latticeSig_neg] at h\n128\t  omega\n129\t\n130\tend SKEFTHawking\n131\t"}]},"uuid":"f42dc980-64e7-46c7-9c59-40556d5e07ed","timestamp":"2026-08-15T19:39:21.287Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e9ec9e2c-80f0-4597-b1fd-04d4f73d426f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/johnroehm/Programming/PythonEnvironments/Physics/Fluid-Based-Physics-Research/SK_EFT_Hawking","sessionId":"6cf37aa2-f1c7-4e33-a712-f17c2d75d99b","version":"2.1.229","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"swift-singing-llama"}

This file discharges that input by assembling the local–global machinery:\n7\t\n8\t* the general-rank diagonal Hasse–Minkowski spine `diag_nary_zero_of_local` (n ≤ 4 bases + n ≥ 5 Meyer\n9\t  descent), with the local hypotheses supplied by\n10\t  - ℝ: `diag_real_isotropic_of_signs` (indefinite ⟹ a `+` and a `−` coefficient),\n11\t  - odd `p`: `PadicSquare.exists_diag_nary_zero_odd_padic` (rank ≥ 5),\n12\t  - `p = 2`: `PadicSquareTwo.exists_diag_nary_zero_2adic` (rank ≥ 5 — the 2-adic frontier),\n13\t* the base-change bridge `matrix_isotropic_congr` (transfer between the Gram matrix and its ℚ-diagonalization),\n14\t* content extraction `exists_int_isotropic_of_rat` (rational ⟹ integer isotropic vector).\n15\t\n16\tThis file currently ships the **rank-≥5 local–global wrapper** `diag_indefinite_rat_zero`; the matrix\n17\tdiagonalization + signature plumbing and the small-rank (2, 4) cases build on top toward the full\n18\t`HasWeakIsotropicVectorHyp`. Kernel-pure, no axioms.\n19\t-/\n20\t\n21\timport Mathlib\n22\timport SKEFTHawking.HasseMinkowskiNary\n23\timport SKEFTHawking.PadicSquareTwo\n24\timport SKEFTHawking.LatticeContent\n25\t\n26\tnamespace SKEFTHawking\n27\t\n28\topen scoped BigOperators\n29\topen QuadraticForm Matrix\n30\t\n31\t/-! ## Signature ⟹ value bricks (for transferring indefiniteness to the diagonalization)\n32\t\n33\t`sigPos > 0`/`sigNeg > 0` of a form means it takes a positive/negative value. The reusable direction we need\n34\t(a positive value forces `sigPos ≥ 1`) is `le_sigPos_of_posDef` applied to the line spanned by that value. -/\n35\t\n36\t/-- A positive value of a quadratic form forces `1 ≤ sigPos` (the line it spans is positive-definite). -/\n37\ttheorem one_le_sigPos_of_pos_value {K M : Type*} [Field K] [LinearOrder K] [IsStrictOrderedRing K]\n38\t    [AddCommGroup M] [Module K M] [Module.Finite K M] (Q : QuadraticForm K M) (x : M)\n39\t    (hx : 0 < Q x) : 1 ≤ sigPos Q := by\n40\t  have hx0 : x ≠ 0 := by rintro rfl; simp at hx\n41\t  have hposdef : (Q.restrict (Submodule.span K {x})).PosDef := by\n42\t    intro v hv\n43\t    obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := Submodule.mem_span_singleton.mp v.2\n44\t    have hvval : (v : M) = t • x := ht.symm\n45\t    have htne : t ≠ 0 := by\n46\t      rintro rfl; simp only [zero_smul] at hvval\n47\t      exact hv (Subtype.ext (by simpa using hvval))\n48\t    show 0 < Q.restrict _ v\n49\t    rw [QuadraticMap.restrict_apply, hvval, QuadraticMap.map_smul, smul_eq_mul]\n50\t    exact mul_pos (mul_self_pos.mpr htne) hx\n51\t  have hfr : Module.finrank K (Submodule.span K {x}) = 1 := finrank_span_singleton hx0\n52\t  calc 1 = Module.finrank K (Submodule.span K {x}) := hfr.symm\n53\t    _ ≤ sigPos Q := le_sigPos_of_posDef Q hposdef\n54\t\n55\t/-- A negative value of a quadratic form forces `1 ≤ sigNeg` (via `sigNeg Q = sigPos (-Q)`). -/\n56\ttheorem one_le_sigNeg_of_neg_value {K M : Type*} [Field K] [LinearOrder K] [IsStrictOrderedRing K]\n57\t    [AddCommGroup M] [Module K M] [Module.Finite K M] (Q : QuadraticForm K M) (x : M)\n58\t    (hx : Q x < 0) : 1 ≤ sigNeg Q := by\n59\t  rw [← sigPos_neg]\n60\t  exact one_le_sigPos_of_pos_value (-Q) x (by rw [QuadraticMap.neg_apply]; exact neg_pos.mpr hx)\n61\t\n62\t/-! ## ℝ → ℚ signature positivity transfer (by density)\n63\t\n64\t`sigPos`/`sigNeg` of a rational form is not known to Mathlib to be scalar-extension invariant, but POSITIVITY\n65\ttransfers ℝ → ℚ by density: a positive (resp. negative) value of the real form gives, via density of `ℚ^m` in\n66\t`ℝ^m` and continuity, a positive (resp. negative) value of the rational form, which forces `sigPos`/`sigNeg`\n67\tof the rational form to be `≥ 1`. This converts the ℝ-stated indefiniteness hypotheses of\n68\t`HasWeakIsotropicVectorHyp` into the ℚ-side input needed to feed the ℚ-diagonalization to the spine. -/\n69\t\n70\t/-- **`sigPos` positivity transfers ℝ → ℚ.** If the real Gram form has `sigPos > 0`, so does the rational\n71\tGram form (a real positive value yields a rational one by density of `ℚ^m`, then `one_le_sigPos_of_pos_value`). -/\n72\ttheorem sigPos_cast_pos {m : ℕ} (A : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) ℤ)\n73\t    (hsp : 0 < sigPos (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap') :\n74\t    0 < sigPos (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℚ)).toQuadraticMap' := by\n75\t  obtain ⟨V, hVdim, hVpd⟩ :=\n76\t    exists_finrank_eq_sigPos_and_posDef (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap'\n77\t  have hVne : V ≠ ⊥ := by rintro rfl; rw [finrank_bot] at hVdim; omega\n78\t  obtain ⟨y, hyV, hy0⟩ := Submodule.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot hVne\n79\t  have hQy : 0 < y ⬝ᵥ (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)) *ᵥ y := by\n80\t    have := hVpd ⟨y, hyV⟩ (by simp [hy0])\n81\t    rwa [QuadraticMap.restrict_apply, toQuadraticMap'_apply] at this\n82\t  set B : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) ℝ := A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ) with hB\n83\t  have hcont : Continuous (fun z : Fin m → ℝ => z ⬝ᵥ B *ᵥ z) := by\n84\t    unfold dotProduct Matrix.mulVec dotProduct; fun_prop\n85\t  have hUopen : IsOpen {z : Fin m → ℝ | 0 < z ⬝ᵥ B *ᵥ z} := isOpen_lt continuous_const hcont\n86\t  have hdense : DenseRange (fun (q : Fin m → ℚ) (i : Fin m) => ((q i : ℝ))) := by\n87\t    have h : (Set.range (fun (q : Fin m → ℚ) (i : Fin m) => ((q i : ℝ))))\n88\t        = Set.univ.pi (fun _ : Fin m => Set.range ((↑) : ℚ → ℝ)) := by\n89\t      ext z; simp only [Set.mem_range, Set.mem_univ_pi]\n90\t      refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩\n91\t      · rintro ⟨q, rfl⟩ i; exact ⟨q i, rfl⟩\n92\t      · intro h; choose q hq using h; exact ⟨q, funext fun i => hq i⟩\n93\t    rw [DenseRange, h]; exact dense_pi Set.univ (fun i _ => Rat.denseRange_cast)\n94\t  obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := hdense.exists_mem_open hUopen ⟨y, hQy⟩\n95\t  have hcast : (fun i => ((x i : ℝ))) ⬝ᵥ B *ᵥ (fun i => ((x i : ℝ)))\n96\t      = ((x ⬝ᵥ (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℚ)) *ᵥ x : ℚ) : ℝ) := by\n97\t    simp only [dotProduct, Matrix.mulVec, hB, Matrix.map_apply]; push_cast; rfl\n98\t  rw [Set.mem_setOf_eq, hcast] at hx\n99\t  have hQqx : 0 < x ⬝ᵥ (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℚ)) *ᵥ x := by exact_mod_cast hx\n100\t  exact one_le_sigPos_of_pos_value _ x (by rwa [toQuadraticMap'_apply])\n101\t\n102\t/-- **`sigNeg` positivity transfers ℝ → ℚ.** Dual of `sigPos_cast_pos` (negative value, via the negated\n103\tform's `posDef` subspace and `one_le_sigNeg_of_neg_value`). -/\n104\ttheorem sigNeg_cast_pos {m : ℕ} (A : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) ℤ)\n105\t    (hsn : 0 < sigNeg (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap') :\n106\t    0 < sigNeg (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℚ)).toQuadraticMap' := by\n107\t  obtain ⟨V, hVdim, hVpd⟩ :=\n108\t    exists_finrank_eq_sigPos_and_posDef (-(A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap')\n109\t  rw [sigPos_neg] at hVdim\n110\t  have hVne : V ≠ ⊥ := by rintro rfl; rw [finrank_bot] at hVdim; omega\n111\t  obtain ⟨y, hyV, hy0⟩ := Submodule.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot hVne\n112\t  have hQy : y ⬝ᵥ (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)) *ᵥ y < 0 := by\n113\t    have := hVpd ⟨y, hyV⟩ (by simp [hy0])\n114\t    rw [QuadraticMap.restrict_apply, QuadraticMap.neg_apply, toQuadraticMap'_apply] at this\n115\t    linarith\n116\t  set B : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) ℝ := A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ) with hB\n117\t  have hcont : Continuous (fun z : Fin m → ℝ => z ⬝ᵥ B *ᵥ z) := by\n118\t    unfold dotProduct Matrix.mulVec dotProduct; fun_prop\n119\t  have hUopen : IsOpen {z : Fin m → ℝ | z ⬝ᵥ B *ᵥ z < 0} := isOpen_lt hcont continuous_const\n120\t  have hdense : DenseRange (fun (q : Fin m → ℚ) (i : Fin m) => ((q i : ℝ))) := by\n121\t    have h : (Set.range (fun (q : Fin m → ℚ) (i : Fin m) => ((q i : ℝ))))\n122\t        = Set.univ.pi (fun _ : Fin m => Set.range ((↑) : ℚ → ℝ)) := by\n123\t      ext z; simp only [Set.mem_range, Set.mem_univ_pi]\n124\t      refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩\n125\t      · rintro ⟨q, rfl⟩ i; exact ⟨q i, rfl⟩\n126\t      · intro h; choose q hq using h; exact ⟨q, funext fun i => hq i⟩\n127\t    rw [DenseRange, h]; exact dense_pi Set.univ (fun i _ => Rat.denseRange_cast)\n128\t  obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := hdense.exists_mem_open hUopen ⟨y, hQy⟩\n129\t  have hcast : (fun i => ((x i : ℝ))) ⬝ᵥ B *ᵥ (fun i => ((x i : ℝ)))\n130\t      = ((x ⬝ᵥ (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℚ)) *ᵥ x : ℚ) : ℝ) := by\n131\t    simp only [dotProduct, Matrix.mulVec, hB, Matrix.map_apply]; push_cast; rfl\n132\t  rw [Set.mem_setOf_eq, hcast] at hx\n133\t  have hQqx : x ⬝ᵥ (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℚ)) *ᵥ x < 0 := by exact_mod_cast hx\n134\t  exact one_le_sigNeg_of_neg_value _ x (by rwa [toQuadraticMap'_apply])\n135\t\n136\t/-- **Local–global for an indefinite integer diagonal form (rank ≥ 5).** A diagonal form `∑ cᵢ xᵢ²` of\n137\trank `n ≥ 5` with nonzero integer coefficients that is indefinite over ℝ (a positive and a negative\n138\tcoefficient) has a nontrivial rational zero. The local hypotheses of `diag_nary_zero_of_local` are all\n139\tautomatic at this rank: ℝ from indefiniteness (`diag_real_isotropic_of_signs`), odd `p` from\n140\t`exists_diag_nary_zero_odd_padic`, and `p = 2` from `exists_diag_nary_zero_2adic`. This is the clean payoff\n141\tof the closed 2-adic frontier — the local input that was missing is now total. -/\n142\ttheorem diag_indefinite_rat_zero {n : ℕ} (hn : 5 ≤ n) (c : Fin n → ℤ) (hc : ∀ i, c i ≠ 0)\n143\t    (hpos : ∃ i, 0 < c i) (hneg : ∃ j, c j < 0) :\n144\t    ∃ x : Fin n → ℚ, x ≠ 0 ∧ ∑ i, (c i : ℚ) * x i ^ 2 = 0 := by\n145\t  apply diag_nary_zero_of_local n c hc\n146\t  · obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := hpos; obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hneg\n147\t    have hij : i ≠ j := by rintro rfl; omega\n148\t    exact diag_real_isotropic_of_signs (fun i => (c i : ℝ)) i j hij\n149\t      (Int.cast_pos.mpr hi) (Int.cast_lt_zero.mpr hj)\n150\t  · intro p _\n151\t    rcases eq_or_ne p 2 with rfl | hp2\n152\t    · exact exists_diag_nary_zero_2adic hn (fun i => (c i : ℚ_[2]))\n153\t        (fun i => Int.cast_ne_zero.mpr (hc i))\n154\t    · exact exists_diag_nary_zero_odd_padic hp2 hn (fun i => (c i : ℚ_[p]))\n155\t        (fun i => Int.cast_ne_zero.mpr (hc i))\n156\t\n157\t/-! ## Rank-≥5 weak isotropy (the main case of [HM])\n158\t\n159\tFor rank `≥ 5`, indefiniteness alone (no even-unimodularity needed) gives an integer isotropic vector:\n160\tdiagonalize over ℚ, transfer indefiniteness to the rational weights (`sigPos/sigNeg_cast_pos` +\n161\t`sig*_of_equiv_weightedSumSquares`), then either a zero weight gives a trivial isotropic vector or the\n162\trank-≥5 local–global wrapper `diag_indefinite_rat_zero` applies to the cleared-denominator integer form. -/\n163\t\n164\t/-- **Weak [HM] for rank ≥ 5.** An indefinite integer Gram form of rank `≥ 5` (positive `sigPos` and\n165\t`sigNeg` over ℝ) has a nonzero integer isotropic vector. Even-unimodularity is *not* required at this rank —\n166\tlocal isotropy over every `ℚ_p` is automatic for rank-≥5 diagonal forms. -/\n167\ttheorem weakIsotropic_of_five_le {m : ℕ} (hm : 5 ≤ m) (A : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) ℤ)\n168\t    (hsp : 0 < sigPos (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap')\n169\t    (hsn : 0 < sigNeg (A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℝ)).toQuadraticMap') :\n170\t    ∃ v : Fin m → ℤ, v ≠ 0 ∧ v ⬝ᵥ A *ᵥ v = 0 := by\n171\t  apply exists_int_isotropic_of_rat A\n172\t  set Aq : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) ℚ := A.map (Int.cast : ℤ → ℚ) with hAq\n173\t  obtain ⟨w, hwe⟩ := QuadraticForm.equivalent_weightedSumSquares Aq.toQuadraticMap'\n174\t  have hspq : 0 < sigPos Aq.toQuadraticMap' := sigPos_cast_pos A hsp\n175\t  have hsnq : 0 < sigNeg Aq.toQuadraticMap' := sigNeg_cast_pos A hsn\n176\t  rw [sigPos_of_equiv_weightedSumSquares hwe] at hspq\n177\t  rw [sigNeg_of_equiv_weightedSumSquares hwe] at hsnq\n178\t  obtain ⟨ip, hip⟩ : ∃ i, 0 < w i := Set.nonempty_of_ncard_ne_zero hspq.ne'\n179\t  obtain ⟨iN, hiN⟩ : ∃ i, w i < 0 := Set.nonempty_of_ncard_ne_zero hsnq.ne'\n180\t  rw [matrix_isotropic_iff_weighted Aq w hwe]\n181\t  by_cases hz : ∃ i, w i = 0\n182\t  · obtain ⟨i0, hi0⟩ := hz\n183\t    refine ⟨fun j => if j = i0 then 1 else 0, ?_, ?_⟩\n184\t    · intro h; have := congrFun h i0; simp at this\n185\t    · rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i0]\n186\t      · simp [hi0]\n187\t      · intro b _ hb; simp [hb]\n188\t      · intro h; simp at h\n189\t  · simp only [not_exists] at hz\n190\t    have hn : Module.finrank ℚ (Fin m → ℚ) = m := Module.finrank_fin_fun ℚ\n191\t    set d : Fin (Module.finrank ℚ (Fin m → ℚ)) → ℤ := fun i => (w i).num * ((w i).den : ℤ) with hd\n192\t    have hdne : ∀ i, d i ≠ 0 := by\n193\t      intro i; rw [hd]; simp only\n194\t      exact mul_ne_zero (Rat.num_ne_zero.mpr (hz i)) (by exact_mod_cast (w i).den_nz)\n195\t    have hdpos : ∃ i, 0 < d i := ⟨ip, by rw [hd]; positivity⟩\n196\t    have hdneg : ∃ i, d i < 0 := by\n197\t      refine ⟨iN, ?_⟩; rw [hd]; simp only\n198\t      have hnum : (w iN).num < 0 := Rat.num_neg.mpr hiN\n199\t      have hden : (0 : ℤ) < (w iN).den := by exact_mod_cast (w iN).pos\n200\t      exact mul_neg_of_neg_of_pos hnum hden\n201\t    have h5n : 5 ≤ Module.finrank ℚ (Fin m → ℚ) := by rw [hn]; exact hm\n202\t    obtain ⟨x, hx0, hxe⟩ := diag_indefinite_rat_zero h5n d hdne hdpos hdneg\n203\t    have hiff := diag_iso_rat_int (K := ℚ) w\n204\t    simp only [Rat.cast_id] at hiff\n205\t    rw [hiff]\n206\t    exact ⟨x, hx0, hxe⟩\n207\t\n208\t/-! ## Small-rank cases (2, and the odd/degenerate vacuities)\n209\t\n210\tThe rank-≥5 case (`weakIsotropic_of_five_le`) covers every form with nonzero signature (an indefinite even\n211\tunimodular form with `σ ≠ 0` has rank ≥ 10). The recursion in `eight_dvd_latticeSig_of_HM_of_Theta` (split\n212\toff a hyperbolic plane, recurse on rank `n-2`) nonetheless reaches the small even ranks `2` and `4`, so the\n213\tweak [HM] hypothesis must hold there too. Rank 2 is elementary. -/\n214\t\n215\t/-- **Even unimodular `2×2` ⟹ `det = -1`.** Pure arithmetic: with both diagonal entries even,\n216\t`det = A₀₀A₁₁ - A₀₁² ≡ -A₀₁² (mod 4) ∈ {0, 3}`, so `det = 1` is impossible; with `det = ±1` this forces\n217\t`det = -1`. (No real-signature input is needed — every even unimodular binary form is indefinite.) -/\n218\ttheorem det_eq_neg_one_of_evenUnimodular_two (A : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℤ) (hA : IsEvenUnimodular A) :\n219\t    A.det = -1 := by\n220\t  obtain ⟨hsym, hdet, heven⟩ := hA\n221\t  have h10 : A 1 0 = A 0 1 := by\n222\t    have h := congrFun (congrFun hsym 0) 1\n223\t    simpa [Matrix.transpose_apply] using h\n224\t  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := heven 0\n225\t  obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := heven 1\n226\t  have hdetval : A.det = A 0 0 * A 1 1 - A 0 1 * A 1 0 := Matrix.det_fin_two A\n227\t  rcases hdet with h1 | h1\n228\t  · exfalso\n229\t    rw [hdetval, ha, hc, h10] at h1\n230\t    rcases Int.even_or_odd (A 0 1) with ⟨k, hk⟩ | ⟨k, hk⟩\n231\t    · rw [hk] at h1\n232\t      have h2 : (2 : ℤ) ∣ 1 := ⟨2 * a * c - 2 * (k * k), by linear_combination -h1⟩\n233\t      norm_num at h2\n234\t    · rw [hk] at h1\n235\t      have h4 : (4 : ℤ) ∣ 2 := ⟨a * c - k * k - k, by linear_combination -h1⟩\n236\t      norm_num at h4\n237\t  · exact h1\n238\t\n239\t/-- **Weak [HM] at rank 2.** Every even unimodular `2×2` form has a nonzero integer isotropic vector. With\n240\t`det = -1` (`det_eq_neg_one_of_evenUnimodular_two`), the explicit vector `![1 - A₀₁, A₀₀]` (or `![1,0]` when\n241\t`A₀₀ = 0`) is isotropic: `vᵀAv = A₀₀·(1 + det) = 0`. -/\n242\ttheorem weakIsotropic_rank_two (A : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℤ) (hA : IsEvenUnimodular A) :\n243\t    ∃ v : Fin 2 → ℤ, v ≠ 0 ∧ v ⬝ᵥ A *ᵥ v = 0 := by\n244\t  have hdneg := det_eq_neg_one_of_evenUnimodular_two A hA\n245\t  obtain ⟨hsym, _, _⟩ := hA\n246\t  have h10 : A 1 0 = A 0 1 := by\n247\t    have h := congrFun (congrFun hsym 0) 1\n248\t    simpa [Matrix.transpose_apply] using h\n249\t  have hdv : A 0 0 * A 1 1 - A 0 1 * A 0 1 = -1 := by\n250\t    have hd : A.det = A 0 0 * A 1 1 - A 0 1 * A 1 0 := Matrix.det_fin_two A\n251\t    rw [h10] at hd; rw [← hd]; exact hdneg\n252\t  by_cases h00 : A 0 0 = 0\n253\t  · refine ⟨![1, 0], ?_, ?_⟩\n254\t    · intro h; have := congrFun h 0; simp at this\n255\t    · simp [dotProduct, Matrix.mulVec, Fin.sum_univ_two, h00]\n256\t  · refine ⟨![1 - A 0 1, A 0 0], ?_, ?_⟩\n257\t    · intro h; have := congrFun h 1; simp at this; exact h00 this\n258\t    · simp only [dotProduct, Matrix.mulVec, Fin.sum_univ_two, Matrix.cons_val_zero,\n259\t        Matrix.cons_val_one]\n260\t      rw [h10]; linear_combination (A 0 0) * hdv\n261\t\n262\t/-- **No `1×1` even unimodular form.** Its determinant `A₀₀` is both `±1` (unimodular) and even, impossible. -/\n263\ttheorem not_evenUnimodular_one (A : Matrix (Fin 1) (Fin 1) ℤ) (hA : IsEvenUnimodular A) : False := by\n264\t  obtain ⟨_, hdet, heven⟩ := hA\n265\t  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := heven 0\n266\t  rcases hdet with h | h <;> rw [Matrix.det_fin_one, ha] at h <;> omega\n267\t\n268\t/-- **No `3×3` even unimodular form.** Reducing mod 2: a zero-diagonal symmetric `3×3` matrix has\n269\t`det = 8abc - 2a·A₁₂² - 2c·A₀₁² + 2·A₀₁A₁₂A₀₂ - 2b·A₀₂²`, every term even, so `2 ∣ det`, contradicting\n270\t`det = ±1`. (This is the rank-3 instance of \"even unimodular ⟹ even rank\", avoiding general char-2\n271\tPfaffian machinery.) -/\n272\ttheorem not_evenUnimodular_three (A : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) ℤ) (hA : IsEvenUnimodular A) : False := by\n273\t  obtain ⟨hsym, hdet, heven⟩ := hA\n274\t  have s01 : A 1 0 = A 0 1 := by\n275\t    have h := congrFun (congrFun hsym 0) 1; simpa [Matrix.transpose_apply] using h\n276\t  have s02 : A 2 0 = A 0 2 := by\n277\t    have h := congrFun (congrFun hsym 0) 2; simpa [Matrix.transpose_apply] using h\n278\t  have s12 : A 2 1 = A 1 2 := by\n279\t    have h := congrFun (congrFun hsym 1) 2; simpa [Matrix.transpose_apply] using h\n280\t  obtain ⟨a, ha⟩ := heven 0\n281\t  obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := heven 1\n282\t  obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := heven 2\n283\t  have hdvd : (2 : ℤ) ∣ A.det :=\n284\t    ⟨4 * a * b * c - a * (A 1 2) ^ 2 - c * (A 0 1) ^ 2 + (A 0 1) * (A 1 2) * (A 0 2) - b * (A 0 2) ^ 2, by\n285\t      rw [Matrix.det_fin_three, ha, hb, hc, s01, s02, s12]; ring⟩\n286\t  rcases hdet with h | h <;> rw [h] at hdvd <;> norm_num at hdvd\n287\t\n288\tend SKEFTHawking\n289\t"}]},"uuid":"3a141909-2384-459b-8a51-fdeb259298c0","timestamp":"2026-08-15T19:41:40.219Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"85b9acb4-e520-4c43-91de-2bf8653b9781","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/johnroehm/Programming/PythonEnvironments/Physics/Fluid-Based-Physics-Research/SK_EFT_Hawking","sessionId":"6cf37aa2-f1c7-4e33-a712-f17c2d75d99b","version":"2.1.229","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"swift-singing-llama"}

{"parentUuid":"611a25a5-c71e-4bc9-bb7b-3caf420d810a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"d7b89794-41f0-46fe-9a20-6d198f8f624f","agentId":"a5ea667e8aa34be02","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01U6CQkiTR9EXY9SArEqoRQH","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/-\n2\tPhase 5c Wave 7A: E8 Lattice Verification\n3\t\n4\tVerifies properties of the E8 Cartan matrix (already in Mathlib as CartanMatrix.E₈):\n5\t  - det(E8) = 1 (unimodular)\n6\t  - All diagonal entries = 2 (even)\n7\t  - Positive definite (σ = 8)\n8\t  - Therefore: E8 is an even unimodular form with σ = 8\n9\t  - This DISPROVES the naive \"algebraic Rokhlin\" claim σ ≡ 0 mod 16 for even unimodular forms\n10\t\n11\tThe algebraic bound is σ ≡ 0 mod 8 (Serre). The jump from 8 to 16\n12\trequires smooth topology (Freedman's E8 manifold has σ = 8).\n13\t\n14\tAll proofs by native_decide or decide — no Aristotle needed.\n15\t\n16\tReferences:\n17\t  Bourbaki, \"Lie Groups and Lie Algebras\" Ch. 4-6, Plate VII\n18\t  Serre, \"A Course in Arithmetic\" Ch. V\n19\t  Freedman, J. Diff. Geom. 17, 357 (1982) — topological E8 manifold\n20\t  Lit-Search/Phase-5c/Rokhlin/Rokhlin's theorem, even unimodular lattices...\n21\t-/\n22\t\n23\timport Mathlib\n24\t\n25\topen Matrix CartanMatrix\n26\t\n27\tnamespace SKEFTHawking\n28\t\n29\t/-! ## 1. E8 Cartan matrix properties -/\n30\t\n31\t/-\n32\tThe E8 Cartan matrix has determinant 1 (unimodular).\n33\tThis is the defining property that makes E8 a unimodular lattice.\n34\t\n35\tPROVIDED SOLUTION\n36\tPROVIDED SOLUTION\n37\tThe 8×8 Leibniz formula has 8! = 40320 terms. Use cofactor expansion\n38\talong row 0 (which has only 2 nonzero entries: E₈(0,0)=2, E₈(0,2)=-1).\n39\tThis reduces to two 7×7 determinants, which further reduce recursively.\n40\tAlternatively, use row reduction: E8 → upper triangular with integer pivots.\n41\t-/\n42\ttheorem e8_det_one : CartanMatrix.E₈.det = 1 := by\n43\t  rw [ ← Matrix.det_transpose ] ; exact by rw [ Matrix.det_apply' ] ; native_decide;\n44\t\n45\t/-- det(E8) is a unit in Z, i.e., det = ±1. -/\n46\ttheorem e8_det_is_unit : IsUnit CartanMatrix.E₈.det := by\n47\t  rw [e8_det_one]; exact isUnit_one\n48\t\n49\t/-- All diagonal entries of E8 are 2 (the \"even\" property).\n50\t    An integral bilinear form Q is even iff Q(x,x) ∈ 2Z for all x,\n51\t    which for Cartan matrices means all diagonal entries are even. -/\n52\ttheorem e8_diagonal_all_two :\n53\t    ∀ i : Fin 8, CartanMatrix.E₈ i i = 2 := by\n54\t  intro i; fin_cases i <;> native_decide\n55\t\n56\t/-- All diagonal entries are even. -/\n57\ttheorem e8_diagonal_even :\n58\t    ∀ i : Fin 8, Even (CartanMatrix.E₈ i i) := by\n59\t  intro i; rw [e8_diagonal_all_two i]; exact ⟨1, rfl⟩\n60\t\n61\t/-- E8 is symmetric: E₈ᵀ = E₈. -/\n62\ttheorem e8_symmetric : CartanMatrix.E₈.transpose = CartanMatrix.E₈ := by\n63\t  native_decide\n64\t\n65\t/-! ## 2. Positive definiteness via leading principal minors (Sylvester's criterion)\n66\t\n67\tThe 8 leading principal minors of E8 are: 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 4, 2, 1.\n68\tAll positive ⟹ positive definite ⟹ σ = 8 (all eigenvalues positive).\n69\t-/\n70\t\n71\t/-- The 1×1 leading minor is 2 > 0. -/\n72\ttheorem e8_minor_1 : CartanMatrix.E₈ 0 0 = 2 := by native_decide\n73\t\n74\t/-\n75\tThe 2×2 leading minor has det = 4.\n76\tThe top-left 2×2 submatrix of E8 is [[2,0],[0,2]] (nodes 0,1 are not connected).\n77\t-/\n78\ttheorem e8_minor_2 :\n79\t    (CartanMatrix.E₈.submatrix (Fin.castLE (by omega : 2 ≤ 8))\n80\t      (Fin.castLE (by omega : 2 ≤ 8))).det = 4 := by\n81\t  native_decide +revert\n82\t\n83\t/-! ## 3. The signature and the Rokhlin counterexample -/\n84\t\n85\t/-- E8 has rank 8 (it's an 8×8 matrix with det = 1 ≠ 0, hence full rank).\n86\t    Combined with positive definiteness, this gives σ = 8. -/\n87\ttheorem e8_rank_8 : CartanMatrix.E₈.det ≠ 0 := by\n88\t  rw [e8_det_one]; exact one_ne_zero\n89\t\n90\t/-- E8 is even (all diagonal entries even) and unimodular (det = 1).\n91\t    Its signature is 8 (positive definite, rank 8).\n92\t    Therefore σ = 8, which is NOT divisible by 16.\n93\t    This DISPROVES the claim \"σ ≡ 0 mod 16 for even unimodular forms.\" -/\n94\ttheorem e8_sigma_not_div_16 : ¬ (16 ∣ (8 : ℤ)) := by omega\n95\t\n96\t/-- The algebraic bound IS σ ≡ 0 mod 8 (Serre's theorem).\n97\t    E8 achieves this bound: 8 ≡ 0 mod 8. -/\n98\ttheorem e8_sigma_div_8 : (8 : ℤ) ∣ (8 : ℤ) := dvd_refl 8\n99\t\n100\t/-- Two copies of E8 give σ = 16 (the Rokhlin bound for smooth spin manifolds).\n101\t    E8 ⊕ E8 is realized by the intersection form of the K3 surface (σ = -16 with sign). -/\n102\ttheorem e8_double_sigma : 2 * (8 : ℤ) = 16 := by norm_num\n103\t\n104\t/-- The key fact: 16 | 2*σ(E8) but NOT 16 | σ(E8).\n105\t    The jump from mod 8 (algebra) to mod 16 (topology) is exactly a factor of 2.\n106\t    This factor of 2 encodes the smoothness constraint (Freedman's counterexample). -/\n107\ttheorem rokhlin_gap :\n108\t    (16 ∣ 2 * (8 : ℤ)) ∧ ¬(16 ∣ (8 : ℤ)) := by\n109\t  constructor\n110\t  · exact ⟨1, by ring⟩\n111\t  · omega\n112\t\n113\t/-! ## 4. The hyperbolic plane H -/\n114\t\n115\t/-- The hyperbolic plane: H = [[0,1],[1,0]]. Even, unimodular, indefinite, σ = 0. -/\n116\tdef hyperbolicPlane : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) ℤ :=\n117\t  !![0, 1; 1, 0]\n118\t\n119\t/-- H has determinant -1. -/\n120\ttheorem hyperbolic_det : hyperbolicPlane.det = -1 := by native_decide\n121\t\n122\t/-- H is symmetric. -/\n123\ttheorem hyperbolic_symmetric : hyperbolicPlane.transpose = hyperbolicPlane := by\n124\t  native_decide\n125\t\n126\t/-- H has even diagonal (both entries 0, which is even). -/\n127\ttheorem hyperbolic_diagonal_even :\n128\t    ∀ i : Fin 2, Even (hyperbolicPlane i i) := by\n129\t  intro i; fin_cases i <;> exact ⟨0, by native_decide⟩\n130\t\n131\t/-- H is unimodular: |det| = 1. -/\n132\ttheorem hyperbolic_unimodular : hyperbolicPlane.det = -1 ∨ hyperbolicPlane.det = 1 := by\n133\t  left; exact hyperbolic_det\n134\t\n135\t/-! ## 5. Classification consequence (Serre/Milnor)\n136\t\n137\tFor INDEFINITE even unimodular forms, the classification is:\n138\t  Q ≅ E₈^a ⊕ (-E₈)^b ⊕ H^c\n139\twith σ = 8(a - b). Since σ is always a multiple of 8,\n140\tthis proves the algebraic Serre theorem: σ ≡ 0 mod 8.\n141\t\n142\tFor DEFINITE even unimodular forms, E₈ (σ=8) is the unique\n143\tpositive definite example in rank 8 (no rank < 8 examples exist).\n144\t-/\n145\t\n146\t/-- The signature formula for the indefinite classification:\n147\t    σ = 8(a - b) where a = #(E₈ copies) and b = #(-E₈ copies). -/\n148\ttheorem classification_sigma (a b : ℤ) : 8 ∣ (8 * (a - b)) := by\n149\t  exact dvd_mul_right 8 (a - b)\n150\t\n151\t/-- Serre mod 8 for E8: σ(E8) = 8, and 8 ≡ 0 mod 8.\n152\t    The full algebraic Serre theorem is in AlgebraicRokhlin.serre_even_unimodular_mod8. -/\n153\ttheorem serre_mod_8_for_E8 : 8 ∣ (8 : ℤ) := dvd_refl 8\n154\t\n155\t/-! ## 6. Module summary -/\n156\t\n157\t/-! ## Module summary\n158\t\n159\tE8Lattice module: verification of E8 Cartan matrix properties.\n160\t  - det(E8) = 1 PROVED (native_decide) — unimodular\n161\t  - All diagonal entries = 2 PROVED — even\n162\t  - E8 symmetric PROVED\n163\t  - Leading minors positive (partial — minor_1, minor_2)\n164\t  - σ(E8) = 8 (positive definite rank 8)\n165\t  - 8 ≡ 0 mod 8 but 8 ≢ 0 mod 16 PROVED — disproves naive algebraic Rokhlin\n166\t  - Hyperbolic plane H: det = -1, even, unimodular PROVED\n167\t  - Classification σ = 8(a-b) PROVED — algebraic Serre bound\n168\t  - All proofs by native_decide/decide/omega — zero sorry, zero axioms, no Aristotle\n169\t-/\n170\tend SKEFTHawking\n171\t"}]},"uuid":"8a23f665-9f4f-4920-99b8-ace41fe9c498","timestamp":"2026-08-15T19:41:48.783Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"611a25a5-c71e-4bc9-bb7b-3caf420d810a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/johnroehm/Programming/PythonEnvironments/Physics/Fluid-Based-Physics-Research/SK_EFT_Hawking","sessionId":"6cf37aa2-f1c7-4e33-a712-f17c2d75d99b","version":"2.1.229","gitBranch":"HEAD","slug":"swift-singing-llama"}
